# Library of provided frameworks

The library provides ready-made templates: the Voluntary Standard VS, pay transparency, and the eleven ESRS templates of the CSRD (ESRS 2 and ESRS E1 to G1, one per standard, to install according to materiality; ESRS 2 always). A framework of your own is not distributed: you import it. This file is produced by `python manage.py bibliotheque` from `referentiel/bibliotheque/socles.py` and `referentiel/bibliotheque/definitions/` (one file per template; check a template with `python manage.py bibliotheque --validate`), which also produces the templates (JSON files) installed from Administration › Frameworks › Add a framework › Install from the library (where you tick the KPIs you want).

- A KPI shared by several base templates has the same code and the same definition everywhere: installing several base templates reuses it (a single value entered, a single catalogue), without conflict.
- Entry levels: the templates have none. On installation, the tool attaches each KPI to the organisation tree, whatever its levels: an inherited parameter (emission factor, conversion factor) at the highest level (a single value, carried over below; exception: the emission factor of electricity, which depends on the country, goes to the Country level if it exists, otherwise to the second level of a tree with three or more levels), an input KPI at the lowest level, unless the template advises the top level (one answer for the company) or the entity level (one answer per legal entity, such as the pay transparency report: the Entity or Subsidiary level, otherwise the level just above the lowest in a tree with three or more levels) (its values roll up through aggregation), a calculated KPI at the highest level of its non-inherited sources; a KPI already present in the installation keeps its level. The level can then be changed in the KPI sheet, or for several KPIs at once in the framework's content.
- KPIs to tick: you can install only part of a template; an unticked formula source is added anyway if a ticked calculated KPI uses it.
- Source documents: each template lists the documents it is based on (title, publisher, version, date of the document, public address, day it was read, licence). The date is the one the document writes; when it writes no exact date, only the year is given. The tool shows the same list on the card of the template, on the installation screens and on the page of the installed framework.
- Units, entry and answer types, description categories: reused if they already exist (same code or same label, same behaviour or same format), otherwise created.
- Annual frequency, comparison with N-1: adjustable after installation (as long as no campaign exists for the frequency).
- Formulas: `[CODE]` same period, `[CODE@N-1]` same period of the previous reporting year. So that a total remains calculable, enter 0 rather than N/A when a source is absent.

## Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module (`VS_BASE`)

Disclosures B1 to B11 of the European Voluntary Standard (Delegated Regulation 2026/1560, which replaces the VSME), for companies with up to 1,000 employees; exports to EFRAG's official workbook (Digital Template 2.0.0). B3, B6 and B7 are voluntary for companies with 10 employees or less (answer N/A).

Template version: 1.2, dated 2026-11-19.

Consistency rules: "Renewable electricity purchased ≤ electricity purchased"; "Female headcount ≤ total headcount"; "Male headcount ≤ total headcount"; "Employees on permanent contracts ≤ total headcount"; "Hazardous waste ≤ waste generated"; "Recycled waste ≤ waste generated"; "Waste prepared for reuse ≤ waste generated"; "Employees covered by agreements ≤ total headcount"; "Water discharged by processes ≤ water withdrawn".

## Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Comprehensive Module (`VS_COMPLET`)

Disclosures C1 to C9 of the European Voluntary Standard, added to the Basic Module (option B of EFRAG's official workbook): strategy, climate targets, climate risks, workforce, human rights, sensitive revenues, gender diversity. C1, C3, C4, the turnover rate (C5), C6 and C7 are largely voluntary for companies with 10 employees or less (answer N/A).

Template version: 1.1, dated 2026-11-18.

## Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting (`TRANSPARENCE_REMU`)

Gender pay gap reporting required by the European directive 2023/970, one report per employer (entered at entity level): mean and median gaps, variable pay, quartiles, gaps by category, joint pay assessment and transparency obligations. Mean gaps are recalculated for the group from the totals; medians and quartiles stay specific to each entity.

Template version: 1.2, dated 2026-11-19.

## ESRS 2 – General disclosures (`ESRS_2`)

General disclosures of the revised ESRS 2: basis for preparation, governance, strategy, materiality assessment, and the general requirements on policies, actions, metrics and targets of entity-specific matters. Install it with the templates of the material topics. Built from Delegated Regulation (EU) 2026/1563 of 3 July 2026.

Template version: 1.1, dated 2026-11-19.

## ESRS E1 – Climate change (`ESRS_E1`)

Revised ESRS E1: transition plan, climate risks, policies, actions and targets, energy, scope 1, 2 and 3 greenhouse gas emissions, removals and carbon credits, internal carbon pricing, anticipated financial effects. Install it if the topic is material, with the ESRS 2 template. Built from Delegated Regulation (EU) 2026/1563 of 3 July 2026.

Template version: 1.1, dated 2026-11-19.

## ESRS S1 – Own workforce (`ESRS_S1`)

Revised ESRS S1: policies, engagement, actions and targets on the own workforce, headcount, non-employees, collective bargaining, gender diversity of top management, adequate wages, social protection, disability, training, health and safety, family leave, remuneration, incidents. Install it if the topic is material, with the ESRS 2 template. Built from Delegated Regulation (EU) 2026/1563 of 3 July 2026.

Template version: 1.1, dated 2026-11-19.

Consistency rules: "Permanent women and men ≤ permanent employees"; "Temporary women and men ≤ temporary employees"; "Permanent and temporary employees ≤ total headcount"; "Non-guaranteed hours employees ≤ total headcount"; "Employees covered by representatives ≤ total headcount"; "Employees below the adequate wage ≤ total headcount"; "Employees entitled to family leave ≤ total headcount"; "People covered by health and safety ≤ own workforce"; "Fatalities from ill health ≤ work-related fatalities of employees"; "Fatalities from ill health ≤ cases of ill health"; "Female headcount ≤ total headcount"; "Male headcount ≤ total headcount"; "Employees covered by agreements ≤ total headcount"; "Employees with disabilities ≤ total headcount".

| G1-5 – Political influence, including lobbying activities | `GOV-CONTRIBUTIONS-DETAIL` | Political contributions by type of recipient | G1-5 · 14 | yes |
| G1-5 – Political influence, including lobbying activities | `GOV-LOBBYING` | Main topics and positions of lobbying activities | G1-5 · 15 | yes |
| G1-5 – Political influence, including lobbying activities | `GOV-NOMINATIONS-PUBLIC` | Members of the bodies appointed from public administration | G1-5 · 16 | yes |
| G1-6 – Payment practices | `GOV-PAIEMENT-CONDITIONS` | Standard payment terms by main category of suppliers | G1-6 · 18(a) | yes |
| G1-6 – Payment practices | `GOV-PAIEMENT-JOURS` | Standard payment terms, in days | G1-6 · 18(a) | yes |
| G1-6 – Payment practices | `GOV-PAIEMENT-JOURS-PME` | Standard payment terms for SME suppliers, in days | G1-6 · 18(a) | yes |
| G1-6 – Payment practices | `GOV-PAIEMENT-PART-CONFORME` | Share of payments aligned with the standard terms, by number of invoices | G1-6 · 18(b) | yes |
| G1-6 – Payment practices | `GOV-FACTURES` | Supplier invoices paid | G1-6 · 18(b) | yes |
| G1-6 – Payment practices | `GOV-FACTURES-A-TEMPS` | Invoices paid within the standard terms | G1-6 · 18(b) | yes |
| G1-6 – Payment practices | `GOV-PAIEMENT-PART-CONFORME-VAL` | Share of payments aligned with the standard terms, by value | G1-6 · 18(b) | yes |
| G1-6 – Payment practices | `GOV-FACTURES-MONTANT` | Amount of supplier invoices paid | G1-6 · 18(b) | yes |
| G1-6 – Payment practices | `GOV-FACTURES-MONTANT-A-TEMPS` | Amount of invoices paid within the standard terms | G1-6 · 18(b) | yes |
| G1-6 – Payment practices | `GOV-PAIEMENT-CONTENTIEUX` | Outstanding legal proceedings for late payment | G1-6 · 18(c) | yes |
| G1-4 to G1-6 – Methods and changes of the metrics | `GOV-CONDUITE-METHODES` | Metrics on business conduct: methods, sources, value chain data and context | G1-4 à G1-6 · ESRS 2 §49(a)-(c) | yes |
| G1-4 to G1-6 – Methods and changes of the metrics | `GOV-CONDUITE-EVOLUTION` | Metrics on business conduct: significant changes and progress | G1-4 à G1-6 · ESRS 2 §49(d) | yes |

Consistency rules: "Invoices paid within the terms ≤ invoices paid"; "Amount paid within the terms ≤ amount paid".

## KPI catalogue

| Code | Name (fr / en) | Unit | Type | Aggregation (organisations / time) | Formula | Definition | Base templates |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NRJ-ELEC` | Électricité achetée consommée / Purchased electricity consumed | kWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Electricity purchased (grid or contract) and consumed on the site during the period. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Include: Offices, warehouses, shops, charging points located on the site. Exclude: Electricity produced and consumed on site (see NRJ-ELEC-AUTO). Source: Supplier invoices or customer account, meter readings. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-ELEC-AUTO` | Électricité renouvelable produite et autoconsommée / Self-generated renewable electricity consumed | kWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Electricity produced on the site (solar panels, wind…) and consumed on site. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Production meter, installer's report. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-ELEC-REN` | Électricité achetée d'origine renouvelable / Purchased renewable electricity | kWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Share of purchased electricity covered by a green tariff with guarantees of origin or by a power purchase agreement (PPA). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Supplier certificate (guarantees of origin). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `NRJ-GAZ` | Gaz naturel consommé / Natural gas consumed | kWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Natural gas consumed on the site (heating, processes, catering), in kWh GCV as on the invoices. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Supplier invoices, meter readings. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-FIOUL` | Fioul consommé / Heating oil consumed | L | Input | Sum / sum | — | Heating oil consumed for heating or generators, in litres. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Delivery notes, tank gauging. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-CHALEUR` | Chaleur et vapeur achetées / Purchased heat and steam | kWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Heat and steam purchased or received, consumed by the site: district heating network, steam from a neighbouring site, waste heat or steam received from a third party's industrial process. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Invoices of the district heating network or of the steam supplier. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-FROID` | Froid acheté (réseau de froid) / Purchased cooling (district cooling) | kWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Cooling delivered by a district cooling network. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: District cooling network invoices. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `CONV-FIOUL` | Contenu énergétique du fioul / Energy content of heating oil | kWh/L | Emission factor | None / last value | — | Energy contained in one litre of heating oil, to convert litres into kWh (about 10 kWh/L). Source: ADEME Base Empreinte or supplier. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-GAZOLE` | Gazole consommé par les véhicules / Diesel used by vehicles | L | Input | Sum / sum | — | Diesel for vehicles and machinery owned or on long-term lease by the entity. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Fuel cards, invoices. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-ESSENCE` | Essence consommée par les véhicules / Petrol used by vehicles | L | Input | Sum / sum | — | Petrol for vehicles owned or on long-term lease by the entity. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Fuel cards, invoices. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-FUGITIVES` | Émissions fugitives (fluides frigorigènes) / Fugitive emissions (refrigerants) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Refrigerant top-ups (air conditioning, refrigeration) multiplied by their global warming potential. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Maintenance log and refrigeration engineer's records. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `FE-GAZ` | Facteur d'émission du gaz naturel / Natural gas emission factor | kgCO2e/kWh | Emission factor | None / last value | — | kgCO2e emitted per kWh GCV of natural gas burnt. Source: ADEME Base Empreinte, DEFRA, IEA or an equivalent national database; keep the same source from one year to the next. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `FE-FIOUL` | Facteur d'émission du fioul / Heating oil emission factor | kgCO2e/L | Emission factor | None / last value | — | kgCO2e emitted per litre of heating oil burnt. Source: ADEME Base Empreinte, DEFRA, IEA or an equivalent national database; keep the same source from one year to the next. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `FE-GAZOLE` | Facteur d'émission du gazole / Diesel emission factor | kgCO2e/L | Emission factor | None / last value | — | kgCO2e emitted per litre of diesel. Source: ADEME Base Empreinte, DEFRA, IEA or an equivalent national database; keep the same source from one year to the next. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `FE-ESSENCE` | Facteur d'émission de l'essence / Petrol emission factor | kgCO2e/L | Emission factor | None / last value | — | kgCO2e emitted per litre of petrol. Source: ADEME Base Empreinte, DEFRA, IEA or an equivalent national database; keep the same source from one year to the next. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `FE-ELEC` | Facteur d'émission de l'électricité (réseau du pays) / Grid electricity emission factor (location-based) | kgCO2e/kWh | Emission factor | None / last value | — | Average factor of the electricity grid in the entity's country ("location-based" method). Source: ADEME Base Empreinte, DEFRA, IEA or an equivalent national database; keep the same source from one year to the next. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `FE-CHALEUR` | Facteur d'émission de la chaleur et de la vapeur achetées / Purchased heat and steam emission factor | kgCO2e/kWh | Emission factor | None / last value | — | Factor of the heat or steam purchased by the site (district heating network, steam supplier); if the site buys both, average factor weighted by the quantities. Source: Network operator, national regulation. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `FE-FROID` | Facteur d'émission du froid acheté / Purchased cooling emission factor | kgCO2e/kWh | Emission factor | None / last value | — | Factor of the district cooling network that supplies the site. Source: Network operator, national regulation. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S1-FIXE` | Émissions de la combustion fixe / Stationary combustion emissions | tCO2e | Calculated | Sum / — | `([NRJ-GAZ] * [FE-GAZ] + [NRJ-FIOUL] * [FE-FIOUL]) / 1000` | Natural gas and heating oil burnt on the site, multiplied by their emission factor. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S1-MOBILE` | Émissions de la combustion mobile / Mobile combustion emissions | tCO2e | Calculated | Sum / — | `([GES-GAZOLE] * [FE-GAZOLE] + [GES-ESSENCE] * [FE-ESSENCE]) / 1000` | Fuels of the entity's vehicles, multiplied by their emission factor. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S1` | Scope 1 : combustion et émissions fugitives (sous-total) / Scope 1: combustion and fugitive emissions (subtotal) | tCO2e | Calculated | Sum / — | `[GES-S1-FIXE] + [GES-S1-MOBILE] + [GES-FUGITIVES]` | Calculated subtotal: stationary and mobile combustion, and fugitive emissions. The other direct emissions (processes, other fuels, agriculture) are entered separately and added to it to make gross scope 1. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S2-LB` | Émissions indirectes liées à l'énergie (scope 2, location-based) / Energy indirect emissions (scope 2, location-based) | tCO2e | Calculated | Sum / — | `([NRJ-ELEC] * [FE-ELEC] + [NRJ-CHALEUR] * [FE-CHALEUR] + [NRJ-FROID] * [FE-FROID]) / 1000` | Purchased electricity, heat, steam and cooling, multiplied by the average factor of their network. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-ACHATS` | Scope 3 – biens et services achetés (cat. 1) / Scope 3 – purchased goods and services (cat. 1) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Emissions from the production of purchased goods and services. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-IMMO` | Scope 3 – biens immobilisés (cat. 2) / Scope 3 – capital goods (cat. 2) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Emissions from the manufacture of equipment, buildings and vehicles acquired. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-ENERGIE` | Scope 3 – énergie, amont (cat. 3) / Scope 3 – fuel- and energy-related activities (cat. 3) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Extraction, production and transport of the energy consumed, transmission losses. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-FRET-AMONT` | Scope 3 – transport et distribution amont (cat. 4) / Scope 3 – upstream transportation and distribution (cat. 4) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Transport of purchased goods to the company's sites. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-DECHETS` | Scope 3 – déchets générés (cat. 5) / Scope 3 – waste generated in operations (cat. 5) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Treatment of the waste produced by the sites. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-DEPLACEMENTS` | Scope 3 – déplacements professionnels (cat. 6) / Scope 3 – business travel (cat. 6) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Trains, planes, rental cars and hotel nights for business travel. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-DOMICILE` | Scope 3 – trajets domicile-travail (cat. 7) / Scope 3 – employee commuting (cat. 7) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Employees' journeys between their home and their workplace. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-FRET-AVAL` | Scope 3 – transport et distribution aval (cat. 9) / Scope 3 – downstream transportation and distribution (cat. 9) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Transport of sold products to customers. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-UTILISATION` | Scope 3 – utilisation des produits vendus (cat. 11) / Scope 3 – use of sold products (cat. 11) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Energy consumed by sold products during their lifetime. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-FIN-VIE` | Scope 3 – fin de vie des produits vendus (cat. 12) / Scope 3 – end-of-life treatment of sold products (cat. 12) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | End-of-life treatment of sold products and their packaging. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `ECO-CA` | Chiffre d'affaires net / Net revenue | M€ | Input | Sum / sum | — | Net revenue for the period, in millions of euros. Source: Consolidated accounts, management control. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `SOC-EFFECTIF` | Effectif total en fin de période / Total headcount at end of period | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Employees on the payroll at the end of the period, all contracts (permanent, fixed-term, work-study), as a headcount. Exclude: Temporary agency workers, interns, contractors. Source: HR information system, payroll. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-EFF-FEMMES` | Effectif féminin en fin de période / Female headcount at end of period | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Women on the payroll at the end of the period. Source: HR information system. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-EFF-CDI` | Salariés en contrat permanent en fin de période / Permanent employees at end of period | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Employees on a permanent contract at the end of the period. Source: HR information system. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-HEURES-FORM` | Heures de formation / Training hours | h | Input | Sum / sum | — | Training hours attended by employees during the period, all formats (in person, remote). Source: Skills development plan, training tool. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-FORM-PAR-SAL` | Heures de formation par salarié / Training hours per employee | h | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-HEURES-FORM] / [SOC-EFFECTIF]` | Training hours divided by total headcount. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-HEURES-TRAV` | Heures travaillées / Hours worked | h | Input | Sum / sum | — | Hours actually worked by employees during the period. Source: Payroll, time management. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-DECES` | Décès liés au travail / Work-related fatalities | people | Input | Sum / sum | — | Deaths of employees during the period as a result of recordable work-related accidents or recordable work-related ill health (sum of both), subject to legal restrictions. Deaths of non-employees are not counted here. Source: Health and safety department. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-HANDICAP` | Salariés en situation de handicap / Employees with disabilities | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Employees who have declared their disability, at the end of the period. Source: HR information system. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PART-HANDICAP` | Part des salariés en situation de handicap / Share of employees with disabilities | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-HANDICAP] / [SOC-EFFECTIF]` | Employees with disabilities divided by total headcount. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `DEC-TOTAL` | Déchets générés / Total waste generated | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Waste produced by the site during the period, all streams. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Waste register, waste transfer notes and service provider reports. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-DANGEREUX` | Déchets dangereux générés / Hazardous waste generated | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Share of waste classified as hazardous (oils, solvents, WEEE, batteries…). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Hazardous waste tracking notes. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `GOV-CORRUPTION` | Condamnations pour corruption / Convictions for corruption or bribery | cases | Input | Sum / sum | — | Final convictions for active or passive corruption handed down during the period. Source: Legal department, compliance. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-AMENDES` | Amendes pour corruption / Fines for corruption or bribery | € | Input | Sum / sum | — | Amount of the mandatory monetary penalties imposed by a court or an administrative or regulatory authority for violation of anti-corruption and anti-bribery laws, paid to a public treasury and recognised in the financial statements of the period (Voluntary Standard VS: B11 §43 and guidance §141; ESRS G1-4 §12 and G1 AR 6, same definition). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Accounting, legal department. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GEN-IDENT-TYPE` | Type d'identifiant de l'entreprise / Entity identifier scheme | list: LEI, DUNS, EU ID, PermID | Input | Count / last value | — | Scheme of the company's unique identifier; EFRAG recommends the LEI. Source: LEI register (GLEIF), D&B, business register. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `GEN-IDENT` | Identifiant de l'entreprise / Entity identifier | written text | Input | None / none | — | Unique identifier of the company in the chosen scheme (for example the 20 characters of the LEI). Required to convert the report to XBRL. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `GEN-INTRO` | Introduction et autres informations propres à l'entreprise / Introduction and entity-specific information | written text | Input | None / none | — | Optional introduction text of the report. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `GEN-CONFORMITE` | Déclaration explicite de conformité / Explicit statement of compliance | written text | Input | None / none | — | Sentence in which the company states that the report complies with the Voluntary Standard, with the module chosen. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `GEN-PERIMETRE` | Base de présentation du rapport / Basis for reporting | list: Sustainability report prepared on an individual basis, Sustainability report prepared on a consolidated basis | Input | Count / last value | — | Report prepared for the company alone (individual basis) or for the group (consolidated basis). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-FORME-JURIDIQUE` | Forme juridique / Legal form | list: private limited liability undertaking, sole proprietorship, partnership, cooperative, other (please specify the legal form in the row below) | Input | Count / last value | — | Legal form of the reporting company. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `GEN-FORME-AUTRE` | Forme juridique (si « autre ») / Legal form (if “other”) | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the legal form is “Other”. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `GEN-NACE` | Codes NACE de l'activité / NACE codes of the activity | written text | Input | None / none | — | NACE code(s) of the activity, one per line, written as 47.91 (the code alone is enough). Source: Business register extract. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `ECO-TOTAL-BILAN` | Total du bilan / Balance sheet total | € | Input | Sum / sum | — | Total assets on the balance sheet at closing date, in euros. Source: Annual accounts. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |

| `GOV-B2-CIBLE` | Cible liée à une politique / Target related to a policy | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company set a target related to one of these policies? | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `NRJ-COMB-AUTRES` | Autres combustibles fossiles / Other fossil fuels | MWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Fossil fuels other than natural gas, heating oil and vehicle fuels (LPG, propane, coal, process gas), in MWh on a net calorific value basis. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Invoices, delivery notes. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-COMB-REN` | Combustibles renouvelables / Renewable fuels | MWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Wood, pellets, biogas, biofuels and other biomass fuels burned on the site, in MWh on a net calorific value basis. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Invoices, delivery notes. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `CONV-GAZ-PCI` | Rapport PCI / PCS du gaz naturel / Net / gross calorific value ratio of natural gas | — | Emission factor | None / last value | — | Gas is billed in kWh of gross calorific value; the standard counts energy on a net calorific value basis. This ratio converts one into the other (about 0.901 for natural gas). Source: ADEME Base Empreinte. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `CONV-GAZOLE` | Contenu énergétique du gazole / Energy content of diesel | kWh/L | Emission factor | None / last value | — | Energy contained in one litre of diesel, to convert litres into kWh (about 10 kWh/L). Source: ADEME Base Empreinte or supplier. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `CONV-ESSENCE` | Contenu énergétique de l'essence / Energy content of petrol | kWh/L | Emission factor | None / last value | — | Energy contained in one litre of petrol, to convert litres into kWh (about 9 kWh/L). Source: ADEME Base Empreinte or supplier. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-COMBUSTIBLES` | Énergie des combustibles / Energy from fuels | MWh | Calculated | Sum / — | `([NRJ-GAZ] * [CONV-GAZ-PCI] + [NRJ-FIOUL] * [CONV-FIOUL] + [GES-GAZOLE] * [CONV-GAZOLE] + [GES-ESSENCE] * [CONV-ESSENCE]) / 1000 + [NRJ-COMB-AUTRES]` | Fossil fuels: natural gas (converted to net calorific value), heating oil, vehicle fuels and other fossil fuels, in MWh. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-TOTAL-MWH` | Consommation totale d'énergie (MWh) / Total energy consumption (MWh) | MWh | Calculated | Sum / — | `([NRJ-ELEC] + [NRJ-ELEC-AUTO] + [NRJ-CHALEUR] + [NRJ-FROID]) / 1000 + [NRJ-COMBUSTIBLES] + [NRJ-COMB-REN]` | Purchased and self-generated electricity, purchased heat, steam and cooling, fossil and renewable fuels (vehicles included), in MWh. Electricity generated on site from a fuel already counted is not added. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S1-AUTRES` | Autres émissions directes (scope 1) / Other direct emissions (scope 1) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Direct emissions not covered by the scope 1 calculation: industrial processes, other fuels (LPG, coal, biomass for CH4 and N2O), agriculture. They are added to the subtotal of combustion and fugitive emissions to make gross scope 1. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S2-MB` | Émissions indirectes liées à l'énergie (scope 2, méthode marché) / Energy indirect emissions (scope 2, market-based) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Scope 2 emissions calculated with the emission factors of the energy contracts: those of electricity (guarantees of origin, power purchase agreements) and also those of the purchased heat, steam and cooling given by the suppliers; for purchased energy without a contractual instrument, the residual mix factor. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-ACTIFS-AMONT` | Scope 3 – actifs loués en amont (cat. 8) / Scope 3 – upstream leased assets (cat. 8) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Emissions of scope 3 category 8. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-TRANSFO` | Scope 3 – transformation des produits vendus (cat. 10) / Scope 3 – processing of sold products (cat. 10) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Emissions of scope 3 category 10. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-ACTIFS-AVAL` | Scope 3 – actifs loués en aval (cat. 13) / Scope 3 – downstream leased assets (cat. 13) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Emissions of scope 3 category 13. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-FRANCHISES` | Scope 3 – franchises (cat. 14) / Scope 3 – franchises (cat. 14) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Emissions of scope 3 category 14. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-INVEST` | Scope 3 – investissements (cat. 15) / Scope 3 – investments (cat. 15) | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Emissions of scope 3 category 15. A category that is not significant: answer N/A (it is not disclosed and does not count in a total). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory (carbon accounting tool, service provider). | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `POL-DECLARATION` | Déclaration des rejets de polluants exigée / Reporting of pollutant emissions required | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Is the company already required to report its pollutant emissions to air, water or soil to the authorities (EU or national law), or does it do so in an environmental management system? | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `POL-PUBLIC` | Rejets de polluants déjà publiés / Pollutant emissions already public | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Is this information already publicly available? | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `POL-URL` | Lien vers la publication des rejets de polluants / Link to the published pollutant emissions | written text | Input | None / none | — | Web address (URL) where this information is published. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `POL-REJETS` | Rejets par polluant / Emissions per pollutant | table: Pollutant, Emissions to air, Emissions to water, Emissions to soil | Input | None / none | — | If the company must already report them (or does so in an environmental management system): one row per pollutant, with its emissions to air, water and soil in kg over the period. Source: Annual emissions report, E-PRTR register. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `POL-REJETS-AUTRES` | Rejets d'autres polluants / Emissions of other pollutants | table: Pollutant, Emissions to air, Emissions to water, Emissions to soil | Input | None / none | — | Pollutants that are not in the European list: one row per pollutant, with its name and emissions in kg. Source: Annual emissions report. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `BIO-SITES` | Sites dans ou près de zones sensibles pour la biodiversité / Sites in or near biodiversity sensitive areas | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Does the company have sites in or near biodiversity sensitive areas (Natura 2000, World Heritage sites, Key Biodiversity Areas)? If yes, each site concerned says so. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `BIO-SITE-ZONE` | Situation du site vis-à-vis d'une zone sensible / Position of the site relative to a sensitive area | list: Located in a biodiversity sensitive area, Located near a biodiversity sensitive area, Neither in nor near a biodiversity sensitive area | Input | Count / last value | — | Is the site in a biodiversity sensitive area, near one, or neither? | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `BIO-SITE-ZONE-NOM` | Nom de la zone sensible proche du site / Name of the sensitive area near the site | written text | Input | None / none | — | Name of the biodiversity sensitive area concerned. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `EAU-PRELEVEMENT` | Eau prélevée / Water withdrawn | m³ | Input | Sum / sum | — | Water withdrawn by the site during the period (mains, borehole, river). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Water bills, meter readings. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E3 – Water |
| `EAU-REJET-PROCESS` | Eau rejetée par les procédés de production / Water discharged from production processes | m³ | Input | Sum / sum | — | Only for a site whose production processes use a lot of water (boilers, drying, washing, cooling, irrigation): water discharged by these processes. 0: no such processes. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Voluntary for a company with 10 employees or less: it can answer N/A. Source: Discharge readings, discharge permit. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `EAU-STRESS` | Eau consommée sur des sites en zone de stress hydrique / Water consumed at sites in water-stress areas | m³ | Input | Sum / sum | — | Water consumed by the site if it is in an area with water stress (for example a baseline water stress of ‘High’ or more in the WRI Aqueduct tool), in cubic metres: water withdrawn minus water discharged from production processes, like the water consumption of the Voluntary Standard VS (B6 §37). The ESRS have their own KPI, which deducts all discharges (E3 AR 4). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Water bills, Aqueduct tool. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `ECO-CIRCULAIRE` | Principes d'économie circulaire appliqués / Circular economy principles applied | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Does the company apply circular economy principles (reuse, repair, recycling, eco-design)? Voluntary for a company with 10 employees or less: it can answer N/A. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `ECO-CIRCULAIRE-DESC` | Application des principes d'économie circulaire / How circular economy principles are applied | written text | Input | None / none | — | Describe how the company applies these principles. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `DEC-RECYCLES` | Déchets redirigés vers le recyclage / Waste diverted to recycling | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Waste of the site sent to recycling, excluding energy recovery and backfilling. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Service provider reports. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-REEMPLOI` | Déchets redirigés vers la préparation en vue du réemploi / Waste diverted to preparation for reuse | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Waste of the site prepared for reuse (checking, cleaning, repair). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Source: Service provider reports. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-PAR-CODE` | Déchets par code / Waste by code | table: Waste code, Waste generated, Of which sent to recycling, Of which prepared for reuse | Input | None / none | — | Optional, in addition to the totals: one row per waste code of the European list, with the mass generated and what was sent to recycling or prepared for reuse, in tonnes. Source: Waste register, waste transfer notes. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `ECO-MATIERES` | Flux de matières utilisées / Material flows used | table: Material, Quantity used, Unit | Input | None / none | — | For a sector with significant material flows (manufacturing, construction, packaging): one row per key material, with the quantity used over the year and its unit. Voluntary for a company with 10 employees or less: it can answer N/A. Source: Purchasing, bills of materials. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `SOC-EFF-HOMMES` | Effectif masculin en fin de période / Male headcount at end of period | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Men on the payroll at the end of the period. Source: HR information system. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-EFF-GENRE-AUTRE` | Effectif d'un autre genre en fin de période / Headcount of other gender at end of period | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Employees who declared another gender, at the end of the period. Source: HR information system. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-EFF-GENRE-NC` | Effectif au genre non communiqué en fin de période / Headcount with gender not reported at end of period | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Employees whose gender is not reported, at the end of the period. Source: HR information system. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-ACCIDENTS-ENREG` | Accidents du travail comptabilisables / Recordable work-related accidents | accidents | Input | Sum / sum | — | Work-related accidents of employees during the period that resulted in death or more than 3 days of absence. Accidents of non-employees (agency workers, self-employed people) are not counted here. Source: Accident register, accident reports. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-SALAIRE-MINIMUM` | Rémunération au moins égale au salaire minimal / Pay at or above the minimum wage | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Do all employees receive pay at or above the applicable minimum wage (law or collective agreement)? | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `SOC-ECART-OBLIGATION` | Écart de rémunération déjà déclaré par obligation / Pay gap already reported by obligation | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Is the company already required to report the gender pay gap (EU or national law)? If yes, it gives the hourly pay below (EFRAG's workbook calculates the gap); if not, it answers N/A to both. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `SOC-REMU-H-HOMMES` | Rémunération horaire brute moyenne des hommes / Average gross hourly pay of male employees | €/h | Input | None / last value | — | Annual gross pay of men (all pay elements included) divided by their hours worked. Method of the Voluntary Standard VS: the gap is calculated in EFRAG's workbook from the two hourly pay figures (B10 §42(b)); ESRS S1 and pay transparency have their own KPIs and their own calculation. Restricted data. Source: Payroll. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `SOC-REMU-H-FEMMES` | Rémunération horaire brute moyenne des femmes / Average gross hourly pay of female employees | €/h | Input | None / last value | — | Annual gross pay of women (all pay elements included) divided by their hours worked. Method of the Voluntary Standard VS: the gap is calculated in EFRAG's workbook from the two hourly pay figures (B10 §42(b)); ESRS S1 and pay transparency have their own KPIs and their own calculation. Restricted data. Source: Payroll. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `SOC-SAL-CONVENTION` | Salariés couverts par une convention collective / Employees covered by collective bargaining agreements | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Employees covered by a collective agreement, at the end of the period (headcount). Source: HR information system. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module, ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `ENV-AUTRES` | Autres informations environnementales propres à l'entreprise / Other environmental entity-specific information | written text | Input | None / none | — | Optional. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |
| `SOC-AUTRES` | Autres informations sociales propres à l'entreprise / Other social entity-specific information | written text | Input | None / none | — | Optional. | Voluntary Standard VS (formerly VSME) – Basic Module |

| `SOC-ECART-DECL-SOUMIS` | Employeur tenu de déclarer cette année / Employer required to report this year | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if the employer must report its pay gaps for this year. Thresholds of the directive: 250 workers or more, every year (first report in 2027 on the year 2026); 150 to 249, every three years (first report in 2027); 100 to 149, every three years (first report in 2031). Fewer than 100: voluntary, unless national law requires it. National law sets the exact dates. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-NB-F` | Femmes prises en compte dans les écarts / Women included in the pay gap calculation | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Number of female workers of the employer included in the calculation of average pay: all contracts, including part-time and fixed-term workers, and agency workers when national law assigns them to the employer. An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-NB-H` | Hommes pris en compte dans les écarts / Men included in the pay gap calculation | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Number of male workers of the employer included in the calculation of average pay, on the same perimeter as women. An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-MOY-AN-F` | Rémunération annuelle brute moyenne des femmes / Average gross annual pay of women | € | Input | None / last value | — | Average gross annual pay of the women included: basic wage or salary and all other components, in cash or in kind (bonuses, variable pay, benefits). Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-MOY-AN-H` | Rémunération annuelle brute moyenne des hommes / Average gross annual pay of men | € | Input | None / last value | — | Average gross annual pay of the men included, with the same components as for women. Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-MOY-HOR-F` | Rémunération horaire brute moyenne des femmes / Average gross hourly pay of women | €/h | Input | None / last value | — | Average gross hourly pay of the women included (the annual pay of each one divided by her paid hours). Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-MOY-HOR-H` | Rémunération horaire brute moyenne des hommes / Average gross hourly pay of men | €/h | Input | None / last value | — | Average gross hourly pay of the men included (the annual pay of each one divided by his paid hours). Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-F` | Femmes qui reçoivent une part variable / Women who receive variable pay | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Number of the women included who received a complementary or variable component during the year (bonuses, commissions, profit-sharing…). Enter 0 if there is none. An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-H` | Hommes qui reçoivent une part variable / Men who receive variable pay | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Number of the men included who received a complementary or variable component during the year. Enter 0 if there is none. An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-VAR-MOY-F` | Part variable annuelle moyenne des femmes qui en reçoivent une / Average annual variable pay of women who receive it | € | Input | None / last value | — | Average gross complementary or variable components of the year, over the women who received one only. N/A if no woman received one. Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-VAR-MOY-H` | Part variable annuelle moyenne des hommes qui en reçoivent une / Average annual variable pay of men who receive it | € | Input | None / last value | — | Average gross complementary or variable components of the year, over the men who received one only. N/A if no man received one. Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-TOT-AN-F` | Total des rémunérations annuelles des femmes / Total annual pay of women | € | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-REMU-MOY-AN-F] * [SOC-REMU-NB-F]` | Calculation data, calculated by the tool: it adds up from one entity to the next, so that the group gap is recalculated from the totals. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-TOT-AN-H` | Total des rémunérations annuelles des hommes / Total annual pay of men | € | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-REMU-MOY-AN-H] * [SOC-REMU-NB-H]` | Calculation data, calculated by the tool: it adds up from one entity to the next, so that the group gap is recalculated from the totals. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-TOT-HOR-F` | Somme des rémunérations horaires des femmes / Sum of the hourly pay of women | €/h | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-REMU-MOY-HOR-F] * [SOC-REMU-NB-F]` | Calculation data, calculated by the tool: it adds up from one entity to the next, so that the group gap is recalculated from the totals. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-TOT-HOR-H` | Somme des rémunérations horaires des hommes / Sum of the hourly pay of men | €/h | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-REMU-MOY-HOR-H] * [SOC-REMU-NB-H]` | Calculation data, calculated by the tool: it adds up from one entity to the next, so that the group gap is recalculated from the totals. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-VAR-TOT-F` | Total des parts variables des femmes / Total variable pay of women | € | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-REMU-VAR-MOY-F] * [SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-F]` | Calculation data, calculated by the tool: it adds up from one entity to the next, so that the group gap is recalculated from the totals. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-VAR-TOT-H` | Total des parts variables des hommes / Total variable pay of men | € | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-REMU-VAR-MOY-H] * [SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-H]` | Calculation data, calculated by the tool: it adds up from one entity to the next, so that the group gap is recalculated from the totals. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-MOY-HOR` | Écart de rémunération moyen (horaire) / Gender pay gap (hourly) | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `([SOC-REMU-TOT-HOR-H] / [SOC-REMU-NB-H] - [SOC-REMU-TOT-HOR-F] / [SOC-REMU-NB-F]) / ([SOC-REMU-TOT-HOR-H] / [SOC-REMU-NB-H])` | Calculated by the tool: difference between the average hourly pay of men and that of women, as a share of men's. Positive when women are paid less. Recalculated at each level from the totals: the group gap is not the average of the entities' gaps. Method of Directive (EU) 2023/970, per employer; the Voluntary Standard VS and ESRS S1 have their own KPIs and their own calculation. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-MOY-AN` | Écart de rémunération moyen (annuel) / Gender pay gap (annual) | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `([SOC-REMU-TOT-AN-H] / [SOC-REMU-NB-H] - [SOC-REMU-TOT-AN-F] / [SOC-REMU-NB-F]) / ([SOC-REMU-TOT-AN-H] / [SOC-REMU-NB-H])` | Calculated by the tool, like the hourly gap, from the average annual pay. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-MED-HOR` | Écart de rémunération médian (horaire) / Median gender pay gap (hourly) | % | Input | None / last value | — | Difference between the median hourly pay of men and that of women, as a share of men's (the median splits the workers into two halves: one earns more, the other less). Entered by the entity: the median of a group cannot be derived from those of its entities. Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-MED-AN` | Écart de rémunération médian (annuel) / Median gender pay gap (annual) | % | Input | None / last value | — | Like the hourly median gap, from the annual pay. Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-VAR-MOY` | Écart moyen sur la part variable / Gender pay gap in variable pay | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `([SOC-REMU-VAR-TOT-H] / [SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-H] - [SOC-REMU-VAR-TOT-F] / [SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-F]) / ([SOC-REMU-VAR-TOT-H] / [SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-H])` | Calculated by the tool: difference between the average variable pay of the men and of the women who receive it, as a share of men's. Recalculated at each level from the totals. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-VAR-MED` | Écart médian sur la part variable / Median gender pay gap in variable pay | % | Input | None / last value | — | Difference between the median variable pay of the men and of the women who receive it, as a share of men's. Entered by the entity. Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-VAR-PART-F` | Part des femmes qui reçoivent une part variable / Share of women who receive variable pay | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-F] / [SOC-REMU-NB-F]` | Calculated by the tool, at each level. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-VAR-PART-H` | Part des hommes qui reçoivent une part variable / Share of men who receive variable pay | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-H] / [SOC-REMU-NB-H]` | Calculated by the tool, at each level. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-QUARTILE-1-F` | Part des femmes dans le quartile 1 (le plus bas) / Share of women in pay quartile 1 (lowest) | % | Input | None / last value | — | Rank the workers from the lowest to the highest pay level and cut the list into four groups of the same size. Share of women in group 1 (lowest). Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-QUARTILE-1-H` | Part des hommes dans le quartile 1 (le plus bas) / Share of men in pay quartile 1 (lowest) | % | Calculated | None / — | `1 - [SOC-QUARTILE-1-F]` | Calculated by the tool: 100% minus the share of women in the same quartile. No value above the entity: the quartiles of a group cannot be derived from those of its entities. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-QUARTILE-2-F` | Part des femmes dans le quartile 2 (inférieur) / Share of women in pay quartile 2 (lower middle) | % | Input | None / last value | — | Rank the workers from the lowest to the highest pay level and cut the list into four groups of the same size. Share of women in group 2 (lower middle). Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-QUARTILE-2-H` | Part des hommes dans le quartile 2 (inférieur) / Share of men in pay quartile 2 (lower middle) | % | Calculated | None / — | `1 - [SOC-QUARTILE-2-F]` | Calculated by the tool: 100% minus the share of women in the same quartile. No value above the entity: the quartiles of a group cannot be derived from those of its entities. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-QUARTILE-3-F` | Part des femmes dans le quartile 3 (supérieur) / Share of women in pay quartile 3 (upper middle) | % | Input | None / last value | — | Rank the workers from the lowest to the highest pay level and cut the list into four groups of the same size. Share of women in group 3 (upper middle). Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-QUARTILE-3-H` | Part des hommes dans le quartile 3 (supérieur) / Share of men in pay quartile 3 (upper middle) | % | Calculated | None / — | `1 - [SOC-QUARTILE-3-F]` | Calculated by the tool: 100% minus the share of women in the same quartile. No value above the entity: the quartiles of a group cannot be derived from those of its entities. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-QUARTILE-4-F` | Part des femmes dans le quartile 4 (le plus haut) / Share of women in pay quartile 4 (highest) | % | Input | None / last value | — | Rank the workers from the lowest to the highest pay level and cut the list into four groups of the same size. Share of women in group 4 (highest). Calculation method: the one set by national law (for example for part-time workers). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-QUARTILE-4-H` | Part des hommes dans le quartile 4 (le plus haut) / Share of men in pay quartile 4 (highest) | % | Calculated | None / — | `1 - [SOC-QUARTILE-4-F]` | Calculated by the tool: 100% minus the share of women in the same quartile. No value above the entity: the quartiles of a group cannot be derived from those of its entities. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-CATEGORIES` | Écarts par catégorie de travailleurs / Gender pay gap by category of workers | table: Category of workers, Women, Men, Gap in basic wage or salary (%), Gap in variable components (%) | Input | None / none | — | One row per category of workers (people who do the same work or work of equal value, grouped by the employer on neutral criteria: skills, effort, responsibility, working conditions). For each category: number of women and men, gap in basic wage or salary and gap in variable components, as a % of the average pay of men. An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). Source: Payroll and job classification. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-CAT-5PCT` | Écart d'au moins 5 % dans une catégorie / Gap of at least 5% in a category | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if the average pay gap between women and men is 5% or more in at least one category of workers. An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-EVAL-CONJOINTE-REQUISE` | Évaluation conjointe des rémunérations requise / Joint pay assessment required | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if a gap of at least 5% in a category is neither justified by objective, gender-neutral criteria nor remedied within six months of the report: the employer must then carry out a joint pay assessment with the workers' representatives. An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-EVAL-CONJOINTE-MENEE` | Évaluation conjointe menée et communiquée / Joint pay assessment carried out and shared | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if the joint pay assessment was carried out with the workers' representatives, then made available to the workers and sent to the monitoring body. N/A if it is not required. An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-CONFIRME` | Exactitude confirmée par la direction / Accuracy confirmed by management | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if management confirmed the accuracy of the information, after consulting the workers' representatives (who have access to the calculation method). An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-TRANSMIS` | Informations transmises à l'autorité / Information sent to the authority | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if all the information of the report (gaps, variable pay, quartiles, categories) was sent to the national authority in charge of collecting it. An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-CAT-DIFFUSE` | Écarts par catégorie communiqués aux travailleurs / Gaps by category shared with the workers | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if the gaps by category of workers were shared with all the workers and their representatives. An employer that does not report this year answers N/A, here and for all the other pay data (otherwise the group total would be wrong). | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-ECART-PUBLIE` | Écarts publiés par l'employeur / Gaps published by the employer | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Optional. Yes if the employer also publishes its gaps (except those by category), for example on its website. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-INFO-CANDIDATS` | Rémunération de départ indiquée aux candidats / Starting pay given to applicants | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if applicants receive, before the interview (for example in the job vacancy notice), the starting pay of the position or its range, and the rules of the collective agreement that apply to it. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-HISTO-NON-DEMANDE` | Aucune question sur la rémunération passée / No question on pay history | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if recruiters never ask applicants about their current or previous pay. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-CRITERES-ACCES` | Critères de rémunération accessibles aux travailleurs / Pay criteria accessible to the workers | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if the workers have easy access to the objective, gender-neutral criteria that set their pay, their pay level and its progression. A Member State can exempt employers with fewer than 50 workers from the part on progression. | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `SOC-REMU-INFO-DROIT` | Travailleurs informés de leur droit à l'information / Workers informed of their right to information | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Yes if, this year, all the workers were informed of their right to know their pay level and the average pay levels, by sex, of their category, and of how to ask for it (written answer within two months). | Pay transparency – gender pay gap reporting |
| `GEN-PERIMETRE-ECART` | Écart avec le périmètre des comptes consolidés : description et raisons / Difference from the boundary of the consolidated financial statements: description and reasons | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the boundary of the own operations used in the sustainability statement differs from the one of the consolidated financial statements: describe the difference and its reasons, for example a subsidiary left out of consolidation because it is not financially material (ESRS 2 §4(a), AR 1). Answer N/A otherwise. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-CHAINE-VALEUR` | Couverture de la chaîne de valeur amont et aval / Coverage of the upstream and downstream value chain | written text | Input | None / none | — | Overview of the extent to which the sustainability statement covers the company's upstream and downstream value chain (ESRS 2 §4(b)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-CONFORMITE-ESRS` | Conformité aux ESRS et version appliquée / Compliance with the ESRS and version applied | list: Revised ESRS (Delegated Regulation 2026/1563), First set of ESRS (Delegated Regulation 2023/2772), First set of ESRS with the reliefs of Article 2(1)(b) of Delegated Regulation 2026/1563, Not fully compliant / voluntary use of the ESRS | Input | Count / last value | — | Statement that the sustainability statement is prepared in accordance with the ESRS applicable at the end of the reporting period, and set of standards applied (ESRS 2 §5). For a financial year starting in 2026, Delegated Regulation (EU) 2026/1563 allows the first set of ESRS, the revised ESRS, or the first set with certain reliefs of the revised ESRS, and asks to state the version applied (Article 2); the revised ESRS apply to financial years starting on or after 1 January 2027 (Article 3). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-DISPOSITIONS` | Allègements, options et dispositions particulières de l'ESRS 1 appliqués / Reliefs, options and specific provisions of ESRS 1 applied | written text | Input | None / none | — | List of the reliefs, options or specific provisions of ESRS 1 that the company applies (ESRS 2 §6). Cases to consider (AR 2): acquisitions and disposals; other time horizons; changes in preparation or presentation; adjusted comparative information; material judgement and significant uncertainty; omission of information; activities excluded from the calculation of a metric; partial boundary of a metric for lack of reliable data; joint operations excluded from environmental metrics; limitations due to undue cost or effort; events after the end of the period; prior-period errors. Answer N/A if none is applied. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-DISPOSITIONS-INFOS` | Informations exigées par les allègements et dispositions appliqués / Information required by the reliefs and provisions applied | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each relief, option or specific provision applied, the information that ESRS 1 requires with it (ESRS 2 §6). It can also be given next to the disclosure concerned (AR 2): then say where. Answer N/A if none is applied. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-LIENS-ETATS-FIN` | Liens avec les états financiers et différences d'hypothèses / Connectivity with the financial statements and differences in assumptions | written text | Input | None / none | — | For the amounts also presented in the financial statements: the cross-reference to the financial statements (ESRS 1 §114); for the amounts that are an aggregation or part of them: how they relate to the most relevant amounts of the financial statements (ESRS 1 §115); and any significant differences between the assumptions of the sustainability statement and those of the financial statements (ESRS 1 §116). Answer N/A if there are none. Source: Accounting, financial statements. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-PROGRESSIF-IRO` | Mesures transitoires : matérialité des sujets omis / Phase-in: materiality of the topics omitted | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company omits all disclosures of one or more topical standards under the phase-in provisions of ESRS 1 (§125(a), §126(a) or §127(a)); answer N/A otherwise. For each topic omitted, say whether its impacts, risks and opportunities were assessed as material by the materiality assessment, and which ones (ESRS 2 §8). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-PROGRESSIF-SUJETS` | Mesures transitoires : sujets importants omis / Phase-in: material topics omitted | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company omits all disclosures of one or more topical standards under the phase-in provisions of ESRS 1 (§125(a), §126(a) or §127(a)); answer N/A otherwise. Topics or sub-topics assessed as material whose disclosures are omitted (ESRS 2 §9(a)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-PROGRESSIF-STRATEGIE` | Mesures transitoires : impacts des sujets omis dans la stratégie / Phase-in: impacts of the topics omitted in the strategy | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company omits all disclosures of one or more topical standards under the phase-in provisions of ESRS 1 (§125(a), §126(a) or §127(a)); answer N/A otherwise. For each material topic or sub-topic omitted, short description of how the business model and the strategy take account of its impacts (ESRS 2 §9(a)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-PROGRESSIF-CIBLES` | Mesures transitoires : cibles datées et progrès sur les sujets omis / Phase-in: time-bound targets and progress on the topics omitted | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company omits all disclosures of one or more topical standards under the phase-in provisions of ESRS 1 (§125(a), §126(a) or §127(a)); answer N/A otherwise.
