| `GEN-PROGRESSIF-BIO-SCIENCE` | Mesures transitoires : cibles sur la biodiversité fondées sur la science / Phase-in: science-based biodiversity targets | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Only if the company omits all disclosures of one or more topical standards under the phase-in provisions of ESRS 1 (§125(a), §126(a) or §127(a)); answer N/A otherwise. Are the targets on biodiversity and ecosystems based on conclusive scientific evidence (ESRS 2 §9(b))? Answer N/A if biodiversity is not a material topic omitted or has no target. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-PROGRESSIF-POLITIQUES` | Mesures transitoires : politiques sur les sujets omis / Phase-in: policies on the topics omitted | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company omits all disclosures of one or more topical standards under the phase-in provisions of ESRS 1 (§125(a), §126(a) or §127(a)); answer N/A otherwise. Short description of the policies on these topics (ESRS 2 §9(c)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-PROGRESSIF-ACTIONS` | Mesures transitoires : actions sur les sujets omis et leurs résultats / Phase-in: actions on the topics omitted and their results | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company omits all disclosures of one or more topical standards under the phase-in provisions of ESRS 1 (§125(a), §126(a) or §127(a)); answer N/A otherwise. Short description of the actions taken to identify, monitor, prevent, mitigate, bring to an end, minimise or remediate the actual or potential negative impacts of these topics, and of their results (ESRS 2 §9(d)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-PROGRESSIF-INDICATEURS` | Mesures transitoires : indicateurs sur les sujets omis / Phase-in: metrics on the topics omitted | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company omits all disclosures of one or more topical standards under the phase-in provisions of ESRS 1 (§125(a), §126(a) or §127(a)); answer N/A otherwise. Metrics relevant to these topics, with their values (ESRS 2 §9(e)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-PROGRESSIF-OMIS` | Autres informations omises au titre des mesures transitoires / Other information omitted under the phase-in provisions | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Does the company omit information under the other phase-in provisions of ESRS 1 (§125(b)-(e), §126(b)-(e), §127(b)-(e): anticipated financial effects, substances of concern and of very high concern)? The standard asks to say so (ESRS 2 §10). If yes, say which in the general part of the statement or in the content index (AR 4). Paragraph 10 covers points (b) to (e) only; the reliefs specific to ESRS S1 (§125(f), §126(f), §127(f): S1-6, S1-7 outside the EEA, S1-10 to S1-12, S1-13 §37(d)-(e) and non-employees, S1-14) are reported in the same way if they are used. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-INDEPENDANTS` | Membres indépendants de l'organe de gouvernance / Independent members of the governance body | people | Input | Sum / last value | — | Independent members of the board (administrative or supervisory body) at period end, counted among all the members of the body (ESRS 2 §12(a)). Source: Minutes and terms of reference of the administrative, management or supervisory body, corporate governance report. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-AUTRES` | Membres de l'organe de gouvernance d'un autre genre ou au genre non déclaré / Members of the governance body of another gender or whose gender is not reported | people | Input | Sum / last value | — | Members of the board (administrative or supervisory body) at period end who are counted neither among the women nor among the men: another gender, or gender not reported (ESRS 2 §12(a)). Enter 0 if there is none; N/A also counts as 0 in the number of members. Source: Minutes and terms of reference of the administrative, management or supervisory body, corporate governance report. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-MEMBRES` | Membres de l'organe de gouvernance, tous genres / Members of the governance body, all genders | people | Calculated | Sum / — | `[GOV-CA-FEMMES] + [GOV-CA-HOMMES] + [GOV-CA-AUTRES]` | Calculated by the tool: women, men, and members of another gender or whose gender is not reported, at period end; an N/A number of other members counts as 0. Denominator of the shares of §12(a) (ESRS 2 §12(a)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-PART-INDEP` | Part des membres indépendants de l'organe de gouvernance / Share of independent members of the governance body | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[GOV-CA-INDEPENDANTS] / [GOV-CA-MEMBRES]` | Calculated by the tool: independent members divided by all the members of the body (women, men, another gender or gender not reported) at period end (ESRS 2 §12(a)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-RATIO-FH` | Mixité de l'organe de gouvernance (rapport femmes / hommes) / Gender diversity of the governance body (ratio of women to men) | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[GOV-CA-FEMMES] / [GOV-CA-HOMMES]` | Calculated by the tool: number of female members divided by the number of male members of the body at period end, as AR 6 requires (ESRS 2 §12(a)). 50% = one woman for two men. This ratio counts women and men only; members of another gender or whose gender is not reported are not in it. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-PART-FEMMES` | Part des femmes dans l'organe de gouvernance / Share of women on the governance body | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[GOV-CA-FEMMES] / [GOV-CA-MEMBRES]` | Calculated by the tool: women members of the body divided by all the members of the body (women, men, another gender or gender not reported) at period end, the percentage by gender of §12(a) (ESRS 2 §12(a)). The ratio of women to men of AR 6, which counts women and men only, is calculated separately. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-PART-HOMMES` | Part des hommes dans l'organe de gouvernance / Share of men on the governance body | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[GOV-CA-HOMMES] / [GOV-CA-MEMBRES]` | Calculated by the tool: men members of the body divided by all the members of the body (women, men, another gender or gender not reported) at period end, the percentage by gender of §12(a) (ESRS 2 §12(a)). The ratio of women to men of AR 6, which counts women and men only, is calculated separately. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-PART-AUTRES` | Part des membres d'un autre genre ou au genre non déclaré dans l'organe de gouvernance / Share of members of another gender or whose gender is not reported on the governance body | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[GOV-CA-AUTRES] / [GOV-CA-MEMBRES]` | Calculated by the tool: members of another gender or whose gender is not reported divided by all the members of the body (women, men, another gender or gender not reported) at period end, the percentage by gender of §12(a) (ESRS 2 §12(a)). The ratio of women to men of AR 6, which counts women and men only, is calculated separately. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-REPRESENTATION` | Représentation des salariés et autres travailleurs dans les organes / Representation of employees and other workers in the bodies | written text | Input | None / none | — | If employees and other workers are represented in the administrative, management or supervisory bodies: how (number of seats, how they are appointed) (ESRS 2 §12(a)). Answer N/A otherwise. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-DIVERSITE` | Autres aspects de la diversité des organes / Other aspects of diversity of the bodies | written text | Input | None / none | — | Breakdown of the members of the bodies by the other aspects of diversity that the company takes into account (age, nationality, background…), as percentages (ESRS 2 §12(a)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |

| `GOV-CA-RESPONSABILITES` | Personnes ou comités responsables des impacts, risques et opportunités / Persons or committees responsible for impacts, risks and opportunities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Identity and responsibilities of the persons, committees or bodies within the governance bodies in charge of managing or overseeing the material impacts, risks and opportunities, as set out in mandates and terms of reference; indicate those that the bodies address themselves without delegating key decisions (ESRS 2 §12(c), AR 8). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-SUIVI-CIBLES` | Cibles : fixation et suivi par les organes / Targets: setting and monitoring by the bodies | written text | Input | None / none | — | How the bodies manage or oversee the setting of targets related to the material impacts, risks and opportunities, and how they monitor progress towards them (ESRS 2 §12(d)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-PRISE-EN-COMPTE` | Prise en compte des impacts, risques et opportunités par les organes / How the bodies take impacts, risks and opportunities into account | written text | Input | None / none | — | How the bodies take the material impacts, risks and opportunities into account in the strategy, the decisions on major transactions and risk management, and how they weigh trade-offs between them if they do (ESRS 2 §12(e)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-ARBITRAGES` | Arbitrages examinés par les organes / Trade-offs considered by the bodies | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Have the bodies or the persons concerned considered the trade-offs associated with the material impacts, risks and opportunities (ESRS 2 §12(e))? | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-REMU-LIEN` | Dispositifs incitatifs liés à la durabilité / Incentive schemes linked to sustainability | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Do the members of the administrative, management or supervisory bodies have incentive schemes linked to sustainability topics? | `GOV-REMU-DURABILITE` | Lien entre la rémunération des organes et la durabilité / Link between the remuneration of the bodies and sustainability | written text | Input | None / none | — | How the remuneration of the members of the bodies is linked to sustainability topics (ESRS 2 §14). | `GOV-REMU-DISPOSITIFS` | Dispositifs incitatifs : caractéristiques principales / Incentive schemes: key characteristics | written text | Input | None / none | — | Description of the key characteristics of the incentive schemes linked to sustainability (ESRS 2 §14(a)). | `GOV-REMU-CRITERES` | Dispositifs incitatifs : cibles ou indicateurs de durabilité utilisés / Incentive schemes: sustainability targets or metrics used | written text | Input | None / none | — | If performance is assessed against specific sustainability targets or metrics: which ones (ESRS 2 §14(b)). Answer N/A otherwise. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-REMU-PART-VARIABLE` | Part de la rémunération variable liée à des cibles de durabilité / Share of variable remuneration linked to sustainability targets | % | Input | None / last value | — | Share of the variable remuneration of the members of the bodies that depends on sustainability targets or metrics (ESRS 2 §14(c)). Source: Remuneration policy of the directors, remuneration report. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-DILIGENCE` | Déclaration sur la diligence raisonnable / Statement on due diligence | written text | Input | None / none | — | Where the sustainability statement reflects the main aspects and steps of the due diligence applied to sustainability topics (ESRS 2 §16); a table that maps the steps to the disclosures is suitable (AR 9). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CONTROLE-INTERNE` | Gestion des risques et contrôle interne du reporting de durabilité / Risk management and internal control over sustainability reporting | written text | Input | None / none | — | Scope, main features and components of the risk management and internal control processes and systems over sustainability reporting, considering the completeness and integrity of the data and the accuracy of estimates (ESRS 2 §18, AR 10). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-MODELE-AFFAIRES` | Modèle d'affaires et chaîne de valeur / Business model and value chain | written text | Input | None / none | — | Description of the business model, of the upstream and downstream value chain and of the company's position in it (ESRS 2 §20(a)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-PRODUITS-MARCHES` | Produits, services et marchés importants, et leur rôle pour la durabilité / Significant products, services and markets, and their role for sustainability | written text | Input | None / none | — | Significant groups of products and services, significant markets or customer groups, their contribution to the company's sustainability objectives, and the significant changes of the period (products, services, markets or customers added or removed) (ESRS 2 §20(b)(i)). Significant: more than 10% of revenue, or connected with actual or potential material impacts (AR 13). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-PRODUITS-INTERDITS` | Produits et services interdits sur certains marchés / Products and services banned in certain markets | written text | Input | None / none | — | Where applicable and significant, products and services banned in certain markets (ESRS 2 §20(b)(ii), AR 13). Answer N/A otherwise. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-SECTEURS` | Secteurs d'activité importants / Significant sectors of activity | written text | Input | None / none | — | Significant sectors of the company's own operations (ESRS 2 §20(c), AR 11 to 13), in the classification chosen: NACE, operating segments of the financial statements (IFRS 8) or another recognised classification. Significant: more than 10% of revenue, or connected with material impacts. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-ACTIVITES-INTERNES` | Activités internes au groupe importantes / Significant activities internal to the group | written text | Input | None / none | — | Activities internal to the group, if they are significant or connected (or may be connected) to material impacts, risks and opportunities; for them, only the connection with material impacts makes a sector significant (ESRS 2 §20(c), AR 13). Answer N/A if there is none. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-ACTIF-FOSSILE` | Active dans le secteur des combustibles fossiles / Active in the fossil fuel sector | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Is the company active in the fossil fuel sector (coal, oil, gas): exploration, extraction, production, processing, storage, refining, distribution, transport or trade (ESRS 2 §20(d)(i), AR 14)? If yes, revenue from coal, oil and gas below. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-ACTIF-CHIMIE` | Active dans la production de produits chimiques / Active in chemicals production | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Do activities of the company fall under Division 20.2 of NACE (pesticides and other agrochemical products) (ESRS 2 §20(d)(ii))? If yes, related revenue below. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-ACTIF-ARMES` | Active dans le secteur des armes interdites / Active in the prohibited weapons industry | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Is the company active in the prohibited weapons industry (anti-personnel mines, cluster munitions, chemical or biological weapons) (ESRS 2 §20(d)(iii))? If yes, related revenue below. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-ACTIF-TABAC` | Active dans la culture et la production de tabac / Active in the cultivation and production of tobacco | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Is the company active in the cultivation and production of tobacco (ESRS 2 §20(d)(iv))? If yes, related revenue below. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-PARTIES-PRENANTES` | Dialogue avec les parties prenantes et parties prenantes clés / Stakeholder engagement and key stakeholders | written text | Input | None / none | — | Summarised description of the stakeholder engagement and of the key stakeholders the company engaged with, by the categories of affected stakeholders of ESRS 1 (AR 23) (ESRS 2 §22(a)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-PP-ATTENTES` | Intérêts et points de vue des parties prenantes clés / Interests and views of key stakeholders | written text | Input | None / none | — | The company's understanding of the interests and views of its key stakeholders in relation to its strategy and business model (ESRS 2 §22(b)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-CA-INFO-PP` | Information des organes sur les points de vue des parties prenantes / How the bodies are informed of stakeholders' views | written text | Input | None / none | — | How the administrative, management or supervisory bodies are informed of the views and interests of the key affected stakeholders, workers' representatives included, on the material impacts, risks and opportunities (ESRS 2 §22(c)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-IRO-INTERACTION` | Impacts, risques et opportunités importants et stratégie / Material impacts, risks and opportunities and the strategy | written text | Input | None / none | — | High-level description: how the material impacts originate from the strategy and business model, what effects the risks and opportunities have on the business model and value chain, and how the company has addressed and plans to address them in its strategy and decisions (ESRS 2 §24, AR 15). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-EFFETS-ACTUELS` | Effets financiers actuels des risques et opportunités importants / Current financial effects of material risks and opportunities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Qualitative and quantitative information (amounts or ranges, or volumes) on how the material risks and opportunities have affected the financial position, financial performance and cash flows of the period (ESRS 2 §25, §32, AR 16 to 20). Quantitative information can be left out in the cases of §28: explain it in the text on information not quantified. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-EFFETS-AJUSTEMENT` | Risque d'ajustement important des valeurs comptables l'an prochain / Risk of a material adjustment of carrying amounts next year | written text | Input | None / none | — | Qualitative and quantitative information on the risks and opportunities for which there is a significant risk of a material adjustment, within the next annual reporting period, to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities of the financial statements (ESRS 2 §26, AR 16). Answer N/A if there is none. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-EFFETS-ATTENDUS` | Effets financiers attendus des risques et opportunités importants / Anticipated financial effects of material risks and opportunities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Qualitative and quantitative information on how the financial position, financial performance and cash flows are expected to change over the short, medium and long term, given the strategy to manage the material risks and opportunities: investment and disposal plans, planned sources of funding (ESRS 2 §27, AR 17 to 21). Phase-in (ESRS 1 §125(b)-(c), §126(b)-(c), §127(b)-(c)): a wave-one company may omit this information for financial years before 2028 (quantitative information: before 2030); another company, for its first two financial years (quantitative information: first four). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-EFFETS-HORIZONS` | Horizons de temps des effets financiers attendus / Time horizons of the anticipated financial effects | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each risk or opportunity, time horizon of the expected change: short term (the length of the financial statements period), medium term (from the end of the short term up to five years) or long term (more than five years), counted from the end of the reporting period (ESRS 2 §27; ESRS 1 §79). Phase-in (ESRS 1 §125(b)-(c), §126(b)-(c), §127(b)-(c)): a wave-one company may omit this information for financial years before 2028 (quantitative information: before 2030); another company, for its first two financial years (quantitative information: first four). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-EFFETS-SANS-CHIFFRES` | Effets financiers : raisons de l'absence d'informations quantitatives / Financial effects: why quantitative information is not given | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company gives no quantitative information on the current or anticipated financial effects of a risk or opportunity (effects not separately identifiable, measurement uncertainty too high, or, for anticipated effects, lack of skills or resources): why (ESRS 2 §28, §29, §31(a)). Answer N/A otherwise. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-EFFETS-COMBINES` | Effets financiers non chiffrés : informations qualitatives et effets combinés / Financial effects not quantified: qualitative information and combined effects | written text | Input | None / none | — | In the same case: qualitative information on these financial effects, with the line items, totals and subtotals of the financial statements affected or likely to be, and quantitative information on the combined effects of this risk or opportunity with others, unless it would not be useful (ESRS 2 §31(b), (c)). Answer N/A otherwise. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `STR-RESILIENCE` | Résilience de la stratégie et du modèle d'affaires / Resilience of the strategy and business model | written text | Input | None / none | — | Qualitative information on the capacity of the strategy and business model to manage the material risks, with how the analysis was conducted and the time horizons considered (ESRS 2 §33). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-MAT-PROCESSUS` | Processus d'identification et d'évaluation des impacts, risques et opportunités / Process to identify and assess impacts, risks and opportunities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Concise description of the process and decision-making steps followed to identify the impacts, risks and opportunities and the related topics, and to assess their materiality: coverage of the own operations and the value chain, key methods, inputs and assumptions, qualitative criteria or quantitative thresholds, dependencies (ESRS 2 §35(a), AR 22 to 24). The tool does not run the assessment: it collects its description. Source: File of the double materiality assessment (method, results, approval). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-MAT-PRIORISATION` | Évaluation et hiérarchisation des impacts / How impacts are assessed and prioritised | written text | Input | None / none | — | How the impacts were assessed and prioritised by severity and likelihood, how actions to prevent, mitigate, bring to an end, minimise and remediate negative impacts are considered, and how areas of heightened risk related to activities, business relationships or geographies were considered (ESRS 2 §35(b), AR 24 to 26). Source: File of the double materiality assessment (method, results, approval). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-MAT-DILIGENCE` | Analyse éclairée par la diligence raisonnable / Assessment informed by due diligence | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Is the assessment of the material impacts, risks and opportunities informed by the company's sustainability due diligence process (ESRS 2 §35(c))? | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-MAT-CONSULTATIONS` | Consultation des parties prenantes et des experts dans l'analyse / Consultation of stakeholders and experts in the assessment | written text | Input | None / none | — | If the company consults affected stakeholders and external experts to understand its impacts: how these consultations inform the assessment (ESRS 2 §35(c), AR 25). Answer N/A otherwise. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-MAT-CHANGEMENTS` | Changements importants du processus d'analyse / Significant changes to the assessment process | written text | Input | None / none | — | Significant changes to the process compared with the previous period (ESRS 2 §35(d)). Answer N/A if there is none. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-MAT-DATE` | Date de la dernière mise à jour de l'analyse de matérialité / Date of the last update of the materiality assessment | written text | Input | None / none | — | Date on which the materiality assessment was last updated, as YYYY-MM-DD (ESRS 2 §35(e)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-IRO-DESCRIPTION` | Description des impacts, risques et opportunités importants / Description of the material impacts, risks and opportunities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Concise description of the actual and potential, positive and negative material impacts and of how they affect or are likely to affect people or the environment, then of the material risks and opportunities with their dependencies; say where they are in the own operations and the value chain (ESRS 2 §37(a), AR 27, AR 28). The next table lists them one by one. Source: File of the double materiality assessment (method, results, approval). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-IRO-TAB` | Liste des impacts, risques et opportunités importants / List of material impacts, risks and opportunities | table: Impact, risk or opportunity, Type, Topic, Place in the value chain, Time horizon | Input | None / none | — | One row per material impact, risk or opportunity: short title, type (actual or potential, positive or negative impact, risk, opportunity), related ESRS topic, place in the value chain, time horizon (ESRS 2 §37(a)). Source: File of the double materiality assessment (method, results, approval). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-CLIMAT-NON-MATERIEL` | Raisons pour lesquelles le changement climatique n'est pas important / Why climate change is not material | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company concludes that climate change is not material and so omits all of ESRS E1: the basis for this conclusion (ESRS 2 §37(b)). Answer N/A otherwise. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-IRO-CHANGEMENTS` | Changements des impacts, risques et opportunités importants / Changes in the material impacts, risks and opportunities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Changes in the material impacts, risks and opportunities compared with the previous period (ESRS 2 §37(c)). Answer N/A if there is none. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-INDEX-EXIGENCES` | Index des exigences de publication appliquées / Content index of the disclosure requirements applied | written text | Input | None / none | — | List of the disclosure requirements applied in preparing the sustainability statement, with where each disclosure is located, indicating separately those incorporated by reference; a table of the requirements with pages or paragraphs is suitable (ESRS 2 §37(d), AR 29). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-INFOS-SUPPLEMENTAIRES` | Liste des informations supplémentaires / List of supplementary information | written text | Input | None / none | — | List of the ‘supplementary’ information disclosed under ESRS 1, section 8.2 (other legislation, other standards or frameworks, non-material information asked for by a user) (ESRS 2 §37(e), AR 29). Answer N/A if there is none. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-RISQUE-TRAVAIL-FORCE` | Exposition au risque de travail forcé et de travail des enfants / Exposure to the risk of forced labour and child labour | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company is connected, through its own operations or its value chain, to material negative impacts related to forced or compulsory labour or child labour: its exposure to a heightened risk of incidents, by type of operations (manufacturing plants, for example) or by country or geography (ESRS 2 §37(f)). Answer N/A otherwise. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-INDEX-DONNEES-UE` | Tableau des points de données issus d'autres textes de l'UE / Table of the datapoints derived from other EU legislation | written text | Input | None / none | — | Table of all the datapoints derived from other EU legislation (Appendix A of ESRS 2: SFDR, Pillar 3, Benchmark Regulation, European Climate Law), with where each one is in the sustainability statement or the mention ‘not material’ (ESRS 2 §37(g)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SANS-PAT` | Sujets importants sans politique, action ou cible / Material topics without policies, actions or targets | written text | Input | None / none | — | Topics related to material impacts, risks or opportunities for which the company has no policies, actions or targets: the standard asks to say so (ESRS 2 §39). They can be marked in the table of impacts, risks and opportunities (AR 31). Answer N/A if there is none. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-POLITIQUES` | Politiques adoptées sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise / Policies adopted on entity-specific matters | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company adopted policies to manage its material impacts, risks and opportunities on entity-specific matters? If not, answer No: the standard asks to say so (ESRS 2 §39). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-POLIT-CONTENU` | Politiques sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : contenu et changements / Policies on entity-specific matters: contents and changes | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each policy: its key contents, its general objectives and the material impacts, risks or opportunities it addresses; describe the changes made during the period (ESRS 2 §42(a)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-POLIT-PERIMETRE` | Politiques sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : périmètre et exclusions / Policies on entity-specific matters: scope and exclusions | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each policy: its scope or exclusions (own operations, upstream and downstream value chain, geographies and, if defined, the affected stakeholder groups) (ESRS 2 §42(b)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-POLIT-REFERENCES` | Politiques sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : normes ou initiatives de tiers / Policies on entity-specific matters: third-party standards or initiatives | written text | Input | None / none | — | Third-party standards or initiatives that the company commits to respect through its policies (ESRS 2 §42(c)). Answer N/A if no policy refers to one. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-POLIT-PARTIES` | Politiques sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : intérêts des parties prenantes concernées / Policies on entity-specific matters: interests of affected stakeholders | written text | Input | None / none | — | How the interests of affected stakeholders were considered in setting each policy, if they were (ESRS 2 §42(d)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-ACTIONS` | Actions clés sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise / Key actions on entity-specific matters | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company taken or planned key actions to manage its material impacts, risks and opportunities on entity-specific matters? If not, answer No (ESRS 2 §39). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-ACT-DESC` | Actions clés sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : description, périmètre et résultats attendus / Key actions on entity-specific matters: description, scope and expected outcomes | written text | Input | None / none | — | Key actions taken during the period and planned, with their timeframe, their scope (own operations, value chain, subsidiaries, geographies, stakeholders) and their expected outcomes, and how they serve the policy objectives (ESRS 2 §45). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-ACT-TAB` | Actions clés sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : calendrier et ressources financières / Key actions on entity-specific matters: timeframe and financial resources | table: Key action, Status, Timeframe, Scope, Capital expenditure of the period, Operating expenditure of the period, Future resources, low estimate, Future resources, high estimate, Depends on preconditions | Input | None / none | — | One row per key action: status, timeframe, scope, capital and operating expenditure of the period, range of future resources, and whether the action depends on preconditions (ESRS 2 §45-46). Amounts only when significant resources are allocated. Amounts in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50). Source: Action plans, budget, accounting (capital and operating expenditure). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-ACT-MOYENS` | Actions clés sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : nature des ressources, conditions et postes comptables / Key actions on entity-specific matters: type of resources, preconditions and line items | written text | Input | None / none | — | When significant financial resources are allocated to the actions: their type (current and future), the preconditions the actions depend on (financial support, public policy, market) and the line items or notes of the financial statements that contain the amounts (ESRS 2 §46). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-CIBLES` | Cibles mesurables sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise / Measurable targets on entity-specific matters | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company set measurable, time-bound and outcome-oriented targets on entity-specific matters? If not, answer No and describe below how it tracks the effectiveness of its policies and actions (ESRS 2 §52). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-CIB-TAB` | Cibles sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : valeurs, années et périmètre / Targets on entity-specific matters: values, years and scope | table: Target, Metric, Absolute or relative, Unit, Base year, Baseline value, Milestone year, Milestone value, Target year, Target value, Required by law, Based on conclusive scientific evidence, Scope | Input | None / none | — | One row per target: metric and unit, absolute or relative target, baseline value and year, milestone, target value and year, whether it is required by law, whether it is based on conclusive scientific evidence (environmental topics), scope (ESRS 2 §51). Source: Company strategy, roadmaps and dashboards. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-CIB-DESC` | Cibles sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : lien avec les politiques, étapes, méthodes et scénarios / Targets on entity-specific matters: link to policies, milestones, methods and scenarios | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each target: its link to the policy objectives and actions, the milestones or interim targets, the methods and significant assumptions and, where applicable, the scenarios, the key data sources and the compatibility with national, EU or international policy goals (ESRS 2 §51(a), (e), (f), (h)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-SUIVI-SS-CIBLE` | Suivi de l'efficacité sans cible sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise / Tracking of effectiveness without targets on entity-specific matters | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if no measurable target is set: whether and how the company still tracks the effectiveness of its policies and actions (ESRS 2 §52). Answer N/A if targets are set. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-METHODES` | Indicateurs sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : méthodes, sources, chaîne de valeur et contexte / Metrics on entity-specific matters: methods, sources, value chain data and context | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each metric: unit, calculation method and sources, estimation method with its significant assumptions and limitations; for a value chain metric, reliance on indirect sources or proxies and the planned improvements; contextual information (ESRS 2 §49(a) to (c)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-EVOLUTION` | Indicateurs sur des sujets propres à l'entreprise : évolutions importantes et progrès / Metrics on entity-specific matters: significant changes and progress | written text | Input | None / none | — | Significant changes in performance compared with the previous periods, progress towards the targets and effect of major acquisitions or disposals (ESRS 2 §49(d)). | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-SPEC-INDICATEURS` | Indicateurs propres à l'entreprise / Entity-specific metrics | table: Metric, Value, Unit, Scope | Input | None / none | — | One row per metric defined by the company for a material topic that the ESRS do not cover, or not with enough granularity: title, value, unit, scope (ESRS 2 §48, AR 42; ESRS 1 §11). A metric to follow by site can also be added as a KPI of the installation. Source: Company dashboards. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-DH-POLITIQUE` | Politique générale des droits humains (ONU, OIT, OCDE) / Overarching human rights policy (UN, ILO, OECD) | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Does the company have an overarching human rights policy that commits to implement the UN Guiding Principles on Business and Human Rights, the ILO Declaration on Fundamental Principles and Rights at Work and the OECD Guidelines for Multinational Enterprises (ESRS 2 §43, AR 34)? | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-DH-PERSONNEL` | Politique des droits humains : personnel de l'entreprise couvert / Human rights policy: own workforce covered | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Does the overarching human rights policy cover the own workforce (ESRS 2 §43)? Answer N/A without such a policy. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-DH-CHAINE` | Politique des droits humains : travailleurs de la chaîne de valeur couverts / Human rights policy: workers in the value chain covered | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Does the overarching human rights policy cover the workers in the value chain (ESRS 2 §43)? Answer N/A without such a policy. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-DH-COMMUNAUTES` | Politique des droits humains : communautés affectées couvertes / Human rights policy: affected communities covered | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Does the overarching human rights policy cover the affected communities (ESRS 2 §43)? | `GOV-DH-CONSOMMATEURS` | Politique des droits humains : consommateurs et utilisateurs finaux couverts / Human rights policy: consumers and end-users covered | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Does the overarching human rights policy cover the consumers and end-users (ESRS 2 §43)? Answer N/A without such a policy. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GOV-DH-POLIT-DESC` | Politique des droits humains : contenu, périmètre et références / Human rights policy: contents, scope and references | written text | Input | None / none | — | For this policy, the information required for any policy: key contents and changes, scope or exclusions, third-party standards or initiatives, consideration of the interests of stakeholders; also name the other stakeholder groups covered (ESRS 2 §43, §42). Answer N/A without such a policy. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |
| `GEN-CHAINE-VALEUR-EFFORTS` | Efforts pour obtenir les informations de la chaîne de valeur / Efforts to obtain value chain information | written text | Input | None / none | — | During the first three reporting years, if not all the necessary information on the value chain is available: efforts made to obtain it, reasons why not all of it could be obtained, and plans to obtain it in the future (ESRS 1 §123). Answer N/A otherwise. | ESRS 2 – General disclosures |

| `CLI-CIBLES-GES-TAB` | Cibles de réduction des émissions de GES / GHG emission reduction targets | table: Target, Scope, Absolute or intensity, Unit, Base year, Baseline value, Milestone year, Milestone value, Target year, Target value, Share of this scope in a combined target (%), Share of the scope inventory covered (%), Gases covered, Entities and areas covered | Input | None / none | — | Absolute scope 1 and 2 targets with a single base year and a single target year are entered in “Absolute scope 1 and 2 targets” above, without repeating them here; this table takes the other targets, and a row for the milestone, the share covered or the gases of an absolute target when there are any. One row per target and per scope: scope (1, 2 location-based or market-based, 3, or combined scopes), absolute target (tCO2e) or intensity target (unit of physical activity or economic output), base year and baseline value, milestones, target year and value. For a combined target: the share of each scope (one row per scope). If the scope of the target (areas, gases, entities) differs from the E1-8 inventory: the share of each scope covered and the gases covered. Targets are gross: no removals, carbon credits or avoided emissions. A target set only in intensity also gives its absolute values for the target year and the milestones (ESRS E1, §24(a)-(b); AR 12 to AR 16). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). A target that is not compatible with 1.5 °C is explained here (ESRS E1, §24(c); AR 2(a), AR 17). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `CLI-CIBLES-EVOLUTIONS` | Cibles de GES : évolutions futures prises en compte / GHG targets: future developments considered | written text | Input | None / none | — | Among the critical assumptions of the targets: how the company has considered future developments (sales volumes, customer preferences and demand, regulation, new technologies) and their possible effect on its emissions and on its emission reductions (ESRS E1, §24(c)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-ACHAT-NUCLEAIRE` | Énergie achetée d'origine nucléaire / Purchased energy from nuclear sources | MWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Nuclear part of the electricity, heat, steam and cooling purchased and consumed by the site during the period, in MWh, according to the energy mix of the supplier or, failing that, of the grid (ESRS E1, §26(b); AR 18). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Contracts and certificates of the energy suppliers (energy mix, guarantees of origin), invoices. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-ACHAT-RENOUV` | Énergie achetée d'origine renouvelable / Purchased energy from renewable sources | MWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Renewable part of the electricity, heat, steam and cooling purchased and consumed by the site during the period, in MWh. Conservative approach: with the market-based method, only energy whose renewable origin is defined in the contract (power purchase agreement, standardised green tariff, guarantees of origin); say in the methods whether the share comes from the market-based or location-based method (ESRS E1, §26(c); AR 18(i)). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Contracts and certificates of the energy suppliers (energy mix, guarantees of origin), invoices. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-ACHAT-FOSSILE` | Énergie achetée d'origine fossile / Purchased energy from fossil sources | MWh | Calculated | Sum / — | `([NRJ-ELEC] + [NRJ-CHALEUR] + [NRJ-FROID]) / 1000 - [NRJ-ACHAT-NUCLEAIRE] - [NRJ-ACHAT-RENOUV]` | Calculated by the tool: purchased electricity, heat, steam and cooling, minus their nuclear and renewable parts, in MWh. Conservative approach: everything that is not shown to be nuclear or renewable counts as fossil (ESRS E1, §27(e); AR 18(i)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-FOSSILE` | Consommation d'énergie d'origine fossile / Energy consumption from fossil sources | MWh | Calculated | Sum / — | `[NRJ-COMBUSTIBLES] + [NRJ-ACHAT-FOSSILE]` | Calculated by the tool: fossil fuels and purchased energy from fossil sources, in MWh (ESRS E1, §26(a)). With nuclear and renewable energy, it makes up the total consumption. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-RENOUVELABLE` | Consommation d'énergie d'origine renouvelable / Energy consumption from renewable sources | MWh | Calculated | Sum / — | `[NRJ-COMB-REN] + [NRJ-ELEC-AUTO] / 1000 + [NRJ-ACHAT-RENOUV]` | Calculated by the tool: renewable fuels, self-generated renewable electricity consumed and purchased energy from renewable sources, in MWh (ESRS E1, §26(c); AR 18). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-CHARBON` | Combustibles : charbon et produits du charbon / Fuel consumption from coal and coal products | MWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Coal, lignite, coke and other coal products burned on the site during the period, in MWh on a net calorific value basis. They are part of the “Other fossil fuels” entered in the calculation data (ESRS E1, §27(a)). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Only for a company with operations in high climate impact sectors (NACE sections A to H and L, glossary); otherwise, answer N/A. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Invoices, delivery notes, meters. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-PETROLE-AUTRES` | Autres produits pétroliers brûlés / Other petroleum products burned | MWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Petroleum products other than heating oil and vehicle fuels (LPG, propane, butane, kerosene, heavy fuel oil…) burned on the site during the period, in MWh on a net calorific value basis. They are part of the “Other fossil fuels” entered in the calculation data (ESRS E1, §27(b)). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Only for a company with operations in high climate impact sectors (NACE sections A to H and L, glossary); otherwise, answer N/A. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Invoices, delivery notes, meters. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-PETROLE` | Combustibles : pétrole brut et produits pétroliers / Fuel consumption from crude oil and petroleum products | MWh | Calculated | Sum / — | `([NRJ-FIOUL] * [CONV-FIOUL] + [GES-GAZOLE] * [CONV-GAZOLE] + [GES-ESSENCE] * [CONV-ESSENCE]) / 1000 + [NRJ-PETROLE-AUTRES]` | Calculated by the tool: heating oil, diesel and petrol converted into MWh, plus the other petroleum products (ESRS E1, §27(b)). Only for high climate impact sectors. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-GAZ-MWH` | Combustibles : gaz naturel / Fuel consumption from natural gas | MWh | Calculated | Sum / — | `[NRJ-GAZ] * [CONV-GAZ-PCI] / 1000` | Calculated by the tool: natural gas billed in kWh of gross calorific value, converted into MWh on a net calorific value basis (ESRS E1, §27(c); AR 18(b)). Only for high climate impact sectors. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-FOSSILE-AUTRES` | Combustibles : autres sources fossiles / Fuel consumption from other fossil sources | MWh | Calculated | Sum / — | `[NRJ-COMB-AUTRES] - [NRJ-CHARBON] - [NRJ-PETROLE-AUTRES]` | Calculated by the tool: “Other fossil fuels” of the calculation data, minus coal and the other petroleum products; for example process gas, hydrogen that is not fully renewable (ESRS E1, §27(d); AR 18(h)). Only for high climate impact sectors. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-PROD-NON-REN` | Production d'énergie non renouvelable / Non-renewable energy production | MWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Energy produced by the site from non-renewable sources during the period (electricity, heat, steam, cooling), whether consumed on site or sold, in MWh. Only if the company produces energy; otherwise, answer N/A (ESRS E1, §28). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Production meters, energy sale contracts. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `NRJ-PROD-REN` | Production d'énergie renouvelable / Renewable energy production | MWh | Input | Sum / sum | — | Energy produced by the site from renewable sources during the period (solar panels, wind, biomass…), whether consumed on site or sold, in MWh. Only if the company produces energy; otherwise, answer N/A (ESRS E1, §28). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Production meters, energy sale contracts. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S1-BRUT` | Émissions brutes de GES du scope 1 / Gross scope 1 GHG emissions | tCO2e | Calculated | Sum / — | `[GES-S1] + [GES-S1-AUTRES]` | Calculated by the tool: stationary and mobile combustion and fugitive emissions, plus the other direct emissions (processes, other fuels, agriculture), in tCO2e, without removals, credits or allowances and without biogenic CO2 (ESRS E1, §30(a)(i); AR 20, AR 21). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S1-SEQE` | Émissions du scope 1 couvertes par le SEQE-UE / Scope 1 emissions covered by the EU ETS | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Emissions of the site's installations covered by the EU Emissions Trading System (EU ETS) during the period, in tCO2e, calculated with the EU ETS method and over the same period as scope 1. Enter 0 for a site without an installation under the EU ETS (ESRS E1, §30(a)(i); AR 21, AR 22). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Verified emissions reports of the EU ETS (Union Registry). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-PART-SEQE` | Part du scope 1 couverte par le SEQE-UE / Share of scope 1 emissions from the EU ETS | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[GES-S1-SEQE] / [GES-S1-BRUT]` | Calculated by the tool at every level: emissions covered by the EU ETS divided by gross scope 1 emissions (ESRS E1, §30(a)(i); AR 22(c)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S1-HORS-GROUPE` | Scope 1 hors du groupe consolidé / Scope 1 outside the consolidated accounting group | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Part of these emissions (gross scope 1) that does not come from the consolidated accounting group (parent and consolidated subsidiaries) but from other operations included in the inventory under the consolidation approach chosen (for example a joint venture under operational control), in tCO2e. Enter 0 if the whole inventory comes from the consolidated group (ESRS E1, §30(c); AR 19). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S2-LB-HORS-GROUPE` | Scope 2 (méthode localisation) hors du groupe consolidé / Scope 2 (location-based) outside the consolidated accounting group | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Part of these emissions (location-based scope 2) that does not come from the consolidated accounting group (parent and consolidated subsidiaries) but from other operations included in the inventory under the consolidation approach chosen (for example a joint venture under operational control), in tCO2e. Enter 0 if the whole inventory comes from the consolidated group (ESRS E1, §30(c); AR 19). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S2-MB-HORS-GROUPE` | Scope 2 (méthode marché) hors du groupe consolidé / Scope 2 (market-based) outside the consolidated accounting group | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Part of these emissions (market-based scope 2) that does not come from the consolidated accounting group (parent and consolidated subsidiaries) but from other operations included in the inventory under the consolidation approach chosen (for example a joint venture under operational control), in tCO2e. Enter 0 if the whole inventory comes from the consolidated group (ESRS E1, §30(c); AR 19). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S1-GROUPE` | Scope 1 du groupe consolidé / Scope 1 of the consolidated accounting group | tCO2e | Calculated | Sum / — | `[GES-S1-BRUT] - [GES-S1-HORS-GROUPE]` | Calculated by the tool: gross scope 1 emissions minus the part outside the consolidated accounting group, in tCO2e (ESRS E1, §30(c)(i)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S2-LB-GROUPE` | Scope 2 (méthode localisation) du groupe consolidé / Scope 2 (location-based) of the consolidated accounting group | tCO2e | Calculated | Sum / — | `[GES-S2-LB] - [GES-S2-LB-HORS-GROUPE]` | Calculated by the tool: location-based scope 2 emissions minus the part outside the consolidated accounting group, in tCO2e (ESRS E1, §30(c)(i)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S2-MB-GROUPE` | Scope 2 (méthode marché) du groupe consolidé / Scope 2 (market-based) of the consolidated accounting group | tCO2e | Calculated | Sum / — | `[GES-S2-MB] - [GES-S2-MB-HORS-GROUPE]` | Calculated by the tool: market-based scope 2 emissions minus the part outside the consolidated accounting group, in tCO2e (ESRS E1, §30(c)(i)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-TOTAL` | Émissions brutes de GES du scope 3 / Gross scope 3 GHG emissions | tCO2e | Calculated | Sum / — | `[GES-S3-ACHATS] + [GES-S3-IMMO] + [GES-S3-ENERGIE] + [GES-S3-FRET-AMONT] + [GES-S3-DECHETS] + [GES-S3-DEPLACEMENTS] + [GES-S3-DOMICILE] + [GES-S3-ACTIFS-AMONT] + [GES-S3-FRET-AVAL] + [GES-S3-TRANSFO] + [GES-S3-UTILISATION] + [GES-S3-FIN-VIE] + [GES-S3-ACTIFS-AVAL] + [GES-S3-FRANCHISES] + [GES-S3-INVEST]` | Calculated by the tool: sum of the significant scope 3 categories, in tCO2e. Enter the emissions of each significant category (by size and the GHG Protocol criteria); a category that is not significant gets N/A: it counts as 0 in this total (“an N/A source counts as 0” box of the KPI sheet, ticked at installation) and is not disclosed as zero (ESRS E1, §30(a)(iii); AR 24, AR 26). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-S3-CLOUD` | Scope 3 – dont informatique en nuage et centres de données / Scope 3 – of which cloud computing and data centre services | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | Part of scope 3 category 1 (purchased goods and services) due to purchased cloud computing and data centre services, in tCO2e. Only if applicable; otherwise, answer N/A (ESRS E1, AR 24(g); AR 26). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `GES-BIOGENIQUE` | Émissions directes de CO2 biogénique / Direct biogenic CO2 emissions | tCO2e | Input | Sum / sum | — | CO2 emitted by the combustion or biodegradation of biomass on the site during the period (wood, pellets, biogas, biofuels), in tonnes, separately from scope 1; the CH4 and N2O of these combustions stay in scope 1 (ESRS E1, §31; AR 20(e)). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Greenhouse gas emissions inventory. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Purchase contracts, cancellation certificates of the carbon credit registries. | ESRS E1 – Climate change |

Answer N/A if there are none. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |
| `CLI-NEUTRALITE` | Allégations publiques de neutralité en GES fondées sur des crédits carbone / Public claims of GHG neutrality based on carbon credits | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company made public claims of GHG neutrality that involve carbon credits? If not, answer N/A to the next two questions (ESRS E1, §35). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E1 – Climate change |

A primary microplastic is a synthetic polymer microparticle that is intentionally produced and added to a product (microbeads, glitter, stabilisers). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable).
