The Voluntary Standard VS has its own KPI, which deducts only the discharge of production processes (B6 §37). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Meter readings, water bills, WRI Aqueduct tool. | ESRS E3 – Water |
| `BIO-POLITIQUES` | Politiques adoptées sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes / Policies adopted on biodiversity and ecosystems | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company adopted policies to manage its material impacts, risks and opportunities on biodiversity and ecosystems? If not, answer No: the standard asks to say so (ESRS 2 §39). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-POLIT-CONTENU` | Politiques sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes : contenu et changements / Policies on biodiversity and ecosystems: contents and changes | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each policy: its key contents, its general objectives and the material impacts, risks or opportunities it addresses; describe the changes made during the period (ESRS 2 §42(a)). Say, where applicable, whether the policies address sustainable land or agricultural practices, sustainable oceans or seas practices, and deforestation (E4 AR 3). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-POLIT-PERIMETRE` | Politiques sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes : périmètre et exclusions / Policies on biodiversity and ecosystems: scope and exclusions | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each policy: its scope or exclusions (own operations, upstream and downstream value chain, geographies and, if defined, the affected stakeholder groups) (ESRS 2 §42(b)). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-POLIT-REFERENCES` | Politiques sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes : normes ou initiatives de tiers / Policies on biodiversity and ecosystems: third-party standards or initiatives | written text | Input | None / none | — | Third-party standards or initiatives that the company commits to respect through its policies (ESRS 2 §42(c)). Answer N/A if no policy refers to one. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-ACTIONS` | Actions clés sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes / Key actions on biodiversity and ecosystems | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company taken or planned key actions to manage its material impacts, risks and opportunities on biodiversity and ecosystems? If not, answer No (ESRS 2 §39). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-ACT-DESC` | Actions clés sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes : description, périmètre et résultats attendus / Key actions on biodiversity and ecosystems: description, scope and expected outcomes | written text | Input | None / none | — | Key actions taken during the period and planned, with their timeframe, their scope (own operations, value chain, subsidiaries, geographies, stakeholders) and their expected outcomes, and how they serve the policy objectives (ESRS 2 §45). The actions take into account the right of indigenous peoples to free, prior and informed consent (E4 AR 6). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-ACT-TAB` | Actions clés sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes : calendrier et ressources financières / Key actions on biodiversity and ecosystems: timeframe and financial resources | table: Key action, Status, Timeframe, Scope, Capital expenditure of the period, Operating expenditure of the period, Future resources, low estimate, Future resources, high estimate, Depends on preconditions | Input | None / none | — | One row per key action: status, timeframe, scope, capital and operating expenditure of the period, range of future resources, and whether the action depends on preconditions (ESRS 2 §45-46). Amounts only when significant resources are allocated. Amounts in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). Source: Action plans, budget, accounting (capital and operating expenditure). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-ACT-MOYENS` | Actions clés sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes : nature des ressources, conditions et postes comptables / Key actions on biodiversity and ecosystems: type of resources, preconditions and line items | written text | Input | None / none | — | When significant financial resources are allocated to the actions: their type (current and future), the preconditions the actions depend on (financial support, public policy, market) and the line items or notes of the financial statements that contain the amounts (ESRS 2 §46). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-CIBLES` | Cibles mesurables sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes / Measurable targets on biodiversity and ecosystems | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company set measurable, time-bound and outcome-oriented targets on biodiversity and ecosystems? If not, answer No and describe below how it tracks the effectiveness of its policies and actions (ESRS 2 §52). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-CIB-TAB` | Cibles sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes : valeurs, années et périmètre / Targets on biodiversity and ecosystems: values, years and scope | table: Target, Metric, Absolute or relative, Unit, Base year, Baseline value, Milestone year, Milestone value, Target year, Target value, Required by law, Based on conclusive scientific evidence, Scope | Input | None / none | — | One row per target: metric and unit, absolute or relative target, baseline value and year, milestone, target value and year, whether it is required by law, whether it is based on conclusive scientific evidence (environmental topics), scope (ESRS 2 §51). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). Source: Company strategy, roadmaps and dashboards. | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-CIB-DESC` | Cibles sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes : lien avec les politiques, étapes, méthodes et scénarios / Targets on biodiversity and ecosystems: link to policies, milestones, methods and scenarios | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each target: its link to the policy objectives and actions, the milestones or interim targets, the methods and significant assumptions and, where applicable, the scenarios, the key data sources and the compatibility with national, EU or international policy goals (ESRS 2 §51(a), (e), (f), (h)). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-SUIVI-SS-CIBLE` | Suivi de l'efficacité sans cible sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes / Tracking of effectiveness without targets on biodiversity and ecosystems | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if no measurable target is set: whether and how the company still tracks the effectiveness of its policies and actions (ESRS 2 §52). Answer N/A if targets are set. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-METHODES` | Indicateurs sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes : méthodes, sources, chaîne de valeur et contexte / Metrics on biodiversity and ecosystems: methods, sources, value chain data and context | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each metric: unit, calculation method and sources, estimation method with its significant assumptions and limitations; for a value chain metric, reliance on indirect sources or proxies and the planned improvements; contextual information (ESRS 2 §49(a) to (c)). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-EVOLUTION` | Indicateurs sur la biodiversité et les écosystèmes : évolutions importantes et progrès / Metrics on biodiversity and ecosystems: significant changes and progress | written text | Input | None / none | — | Significant changes in performance compared with the previous periods, progress towards the targets and effect of major acquisitions or disposals (ESRS 2 §49(d)). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-PLAN` | Plan de transition pour la biodiversité en place et public / Biodiversity transition plan in place and made public | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Does the company have a transition plan that transforms its business model and strategy to contribute to halting and reversing biodiversity loss (Kunming-Montreal Global Biodiversity Framework), and has it made the key features public? ‘Made public’: available before or at the release of the sustainability statement, for example on its website or in its reports. A climate plan that integrates biodiversity may do. If yes, describe the plan below (E4-1 §11, E4 AR 1, AR 2). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-PLAN-TRANSITION` | Caractéristiques clés du plan de transition pour la biodiversité / Key features of the biodiversity transition plan | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the plan exists and is public: its key features (E4-1 §11). Such a plan usually contains targets, key actions, financial planning and governance, and explains how the strategy and business model will evolve; it can address synergies and trade-offs with other transition plan objectives (E4 AR 1). Answer N/A if there is no public plan. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-POLIT-TRACABILITE` | Politiques sur la biodiversité : traçabilité dans la chaîne de valeur / Biodiversity policies: traceability in the value chain | written text | Input | None / none | — | How the biodiversity and ecosystems policies support the traceability of the products, components and raw materials that have, or could have, material impacts on biodiversity in the value chain (E4-2 §13(a)). Answer N/A if there is no policy on this point. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-POLIT-SITES` | Politiques sur la biodiversité : sites dans ou près d'une zone sensible / Biodiversity policies: sites in or near sensitive areas | written text | Input | None / none | — | What the biodiversity and ecosystems policies provide for the sites of the own operations in or near a biodiversity-sensitive area (E4-2 §13(b)). A site outside an area is ‘near’ it according to its area of influence, which the company defines for example with buffer distances specific to its type of activity (E4 AR 5). Answer N/A if no site is concerned. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-COMPENSATIONS` | Compensations de biodiversité utilisées / Biodiversity offsets used | written text | Input | None / none | — | The biodiversity offsets used in the actions: their aim, the financing effects in monetary terms, the area, type and quality criteria applied, and the standards they comply with (E4-3 §15). An offset is a measurable conservation outcome that compensates for unavoidable negative impacts, in addition to prevention and mitigation (glossary). Write the amounts and areas in the text. Answer N/A if there are none. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-CIBLES-COMPENSATIONS` | Compensations de biodiversité utilisées dans les cibles / Use of biodiversity offsets in the targets | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company uses biodiversity offsets in setting its targets: how it used them (E4-4 §17). Answer N/A otherwise. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-LIEUX-DESC` | Lieux des activités propres liés à des enjeux de biodiversité importants / Locations of the own operations linked to material biodiversity matters | written text | Input | None / none | — | The locations of the own operations to which the material biodiversity and ecosystems impacts, risks or opportunities relate, when they are not sites of the organisation tree (E4-5 §19(a), E4 AR 8). Each site of the tree answers with the yes / no ‘site concerned’. No exhaustive list is needed: the company may group the sites that affect the same sensitive area or have similar impacts, risks or opportunities, as long as this does not hide material information. Answer N/A if all the locations are sites of the tree. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-SITE-IRO` | Site lié à un impact, risque ou opportunité important pour la biodiversité / Site linked to a material biodiversity impact, risk or opportunity | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Is the site a location to which material biodiversity and ecosystems impacts, risks or opportunities relate (E4-5 §19(a), E4 AR 8)? If yes, fill in the next two texts. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-SITE-ZONE-IMPACT` | Zones sensibles touchées par le site (nom et type) / Sensitive areas affected by the site (name and type) | written text | Input | None / none | — | For a site concerned: the list of the biodiversity-sensitive areas related to its material negative impacts, with the name and type of each area (E4-5 §19(b)). Sensitive areas: protected areas (Natura 2000, UNESCO natural world heritage sites, Ramsar sites…), Key Biodiversity Areas, ecologically or biologically significant marine areas, habitats of species of the IUCN Red List (glossary). Answer N/A if the site is not concerned or has no material negative impact on a sensitive area. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). Source: Impact assessment, operating permits, maps of protected areas, site operating records. | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-SITE-ACTIVITES` | Activités du site liées à des impacts négatifs sur des zones sensibles / Activities of the site linked to negative impacts on sensitive areas | written text | Input | None / none | — | For a site that has a material negative impact on a sensitive area: the activities of the site that are linked to this impact (E4-5 §19(c)). Answer N/A otherwise. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). Source: Impact assessment, operating permits, maps of protected areas, site operating records. | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-INDICATEURS` | Indicateurs sur les impacts importants sur la biodiversité / Metrics on the material impacts on biodiversity | table: Metric, Value, Unit, Scope | Input | None / none | — | One row per company metric on its material impacts on biodiversity and ecosystems: name, value, unit, scope (E4-5 §20). The standard defines no metric. Depending on the material sub-topic, they cover drivers of change, the state of species, the condition and extent of ecosystems or ecosystem services (E4 AR 10). Primary data from the field or remote sensing is best; otherwise estimates from pressure data (E4 AR 11). Say in the methods text the monitoring frequency, the baseline condition and the baseline year (E4 AR 12). Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). Source: Ecological monitoring, field surveys, satellite imagery, impact assessments. | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-IMPACT-SOLS` | Impacts importants liés à la dégradation des terres, la désertification ou l'imperméabilisation des sols / Material impacts related to land degradation, desertification or soil sealing | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Among the material impacts described in the assessment of impacts, risks and opportunities, do any concern land degradation, desertification or soil sealing (E4 AR 9, for E4-5 §20)? If yes, the text on the material impacts (ESRS 2 IRO-2) says so. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |
| `BIO-IMPACT-ESPECES` | Activités touchant des espèces ou des écosystèmes menacés / Operations that affect threatened species or ecosystems | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Among the material impacts, do any concern operations that affect threatened species or ecosystems (E4 AR 9, for E4-5 §20)? Threatened species: endangered species of the European Red List or the IUCN Red List of Threatened Species; threatened ecosystems: ecosystems classed as critically endangered, endangered or vulnerable in the IUCN Red List of Ecosystems (glossary). If yes, the text on the material impacts (ESRS 2 IRO-2) says so. Phase-in: a wave-one company may omit the whole of ESRS E4 for financial years starting before 2027, and any other company for its first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a), §127(a)). | ESRS E4 – Biodiversity and ecosystems |

| `ECO-CIRC-METHODES` | Indicateurs sur les ressources et l'économie circulaire : méthodes, sources, chaîne de valeur et contexte / Metrics on resource use and circular economy: methods, sources, value chain data and context | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each metric: unit, calculation method and sources, estimation method with its significant assumptions and limitations; for a value chain metric, reliance on indirect sources or proxies and the planned improvements; contextual information (ESRS 2 §49(a) to (c)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `ECO-CIRC-EVOLUTION` | Indicateurs sur les ressources et l'économie circulaire : évolutions importantes et progrès / Metrics on resource use and circular economy: significant changes and progress | written text | Input | None / none | — | Significant changes in performance compared with the previous periods, progress towards the targets and effect of major acquisitions or disposals (ESRS 2 §49(d)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `ECO-CIRC-ECOCONCEPTION` | Principes d'économie circulaire et d'écoconception dans les produits et services clés / Circular economy principles and eco-design in key products and services | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company integrates circular economy principles or eco-design requirements in its key products and circular economy services: explain how (E5-1 §9). Circular economy principles: eliminate waste and pollution by design, circulate products and materials at their highest value through maintenance, reuse and refurbishment, regenerate nature (glossary). Key product: a product central to value creation or strategy, defined by managerial assessment (glossary). Answer N/A otherwise. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `ECO-CIRC-MATIERES-DESC` | Matières clés, matières premières critiques et stratégiques / Key materials, critical and strategic raw materials | written text | Input | None / none | — | A short description of each key material used, with its role and relevance in the operations, and the critical or strategic raw materials it contains (E5-4 §13(a), E5 AR 1, AR 2). Key material: a material, substance, component or product essential to the ability to manufacture or deliver, identified by managerial assessment (volume, cost, criticality, external factors) (glossary). Tell technical material from biological material if it helps to understand the impacts, risks or opportunities; point out the critical and strategic raw materials of Regulation (EU) 2024/1252 (Annexes I and II), for example a battery that contains lithium. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `ECO-CIRC-MATIERES-POIDS` | Poids total des matières clés / Total weight of key materials | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Weight of all the key materials that entered the operations of the site during the period, in tonnes (E5-4 §13(b)). Also the total for the share of secondary resources. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Purchasing, bills of materials, material balances. | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `ECO-CIRC-MATIERES-TAB` | Poids de chaque matière clé / Weight of each key material | table: Key material, Weight | Input | None / none | — | One row per key material: its weight in tonnes over the period (E5-4 §13(c)). The standard allows the weight or the share of the total weight: enter the weight, the share follows from the total weight of key materials. The total of the table must be the total weight of key materials. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Purchasing, bills of materials, material balances. | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `ECO-CIRC-SECONDAIRES` | Poids des ressources secondaires utilisées / Weight of secondary resources used | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Weight of the secondary resources that entered the operations of the site during the period, in tonnes, among the key materials (E5-4 §13(d)). Secondary resource: a material previously used, recovered from waste streams or other sources, and reintroduced into production cycles, for example recycled material, reused, repaired, refurbished or remanufactured product or component (glossary). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Purchasing, recycled-content sheets, material balances. | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `ECO-CIRC-PART-SECONDAIRES` | Part des ressources secondaires dans les matières clés / Share of secondary resources in key materials | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[ECO-CIRC-SECONDAIRES] / [ECO-CIRC-MATIERES-POIDS]` | Calculated by the tool: weight of secondary resources divided by the total weight of key materials (E5-4 §13(d), presentation as a share). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `ECO-CIRC-DURABILITE` | Durabilité attendue des produits clés / Expected durability of key products | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each key product: its expected durability, as qualitative or quantitative information (E5-5 §15(a)). If quantitative, write the measure and its unit, for example a lifetime in years or cycles, and how it is established. Key product: see the glossary. Answer N/A if there is no key product. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `ECO-CIRC-REPARABILITE` | Réparabilité des produits clés / Repairability of key products | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each key product: the extent to which it is repairable, as qualitative or quantitative information (E5-5 §15(b)). If quantitative, write the measure and its unit, for example a repairability index, and how it is established. Answer N/A if there is no key product. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `ECO-CIRC-RECYCLABILITE` | Taux de recyclabilité prévu des produits clés et de leurs emballages / Designed recyclability rate of key products and their packaging | table: Key product, Designed recyclability rate of the product (%), Designed recyclability rate of the packaging (%) | Input | None / none | — | One row per key product: the recyclability rate designed into the product and that of its packaging, as a percentage from 0 to 100 (E5-5 §15(c), E5 AR 3). Rate of a product = total weight of recyclable materials incorporated in the product ÷ total weight of the product × 100; same calculation for the packaging, with the weight of the packaging. Answer N/A if there is no key product. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Product sheets, bills of materials, recyclability studies. | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-FLUX-DESC` | Flux de déchets / Waste streams | written text | Input | None / none | — | A description of the waste streams of the own operations: for each stream, a name (for example from the European List of Waste, Decision 2000/532/EC) and the key materials it contains (biomass, metals, non-metallic minerals, plastics, textiles, critical raw materials, rare earths…) (E5-5 §16(a), E5 AR 4). Also write what the ‘other recovery operations’ used are (E5 AR 6). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-AUTRE-VALO` | Déchets envoyés vers d'autres opérations de valorisation / Waste sent to other recovery operations | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Waste of the site sent to recovery operations other than preparation for reuse and recycling, during the period, in tonnes (E5-5 §16(c)(iii), E5 AR 6). Recovery operations: Annex II to Directive 2008/98/EC on waste. Incineration with energy recovery is one only if it meets condition R1 of that Annex; otherwise it is incineration (disposal). Say which ones in the description of the waste streams. Weight of the waste in its original state, with no further manipulation such as a dry weight (E5 AR 5). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Waste register, waste transfer notes, service provider reports. | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-DETOURNES` | Déchets détournés de l'élimination (réemploi, recyclage, autres valorisations) / Waste diverted from disposal (reuse, recycling, other recovery) | t | Calculated | Sum / — | `[DEC-REEMPLOI] + [DEC-RECYCLES] + [DEC-AUTRE-VALO]` | Calculated by the tool: waste prepared for reuse, recycled and sent to other recovery operations (E5-5 §16(c)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-DETOURNES-DANG` | Déchets dangereux détournés de l'élimination / Hazardous waste diverted from disposal | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Part of the waste diverted from disposal (reuse, recycling, other recovery) that is hazardous, during the period, in tonnes (E5-5 §16(c)). Hazardous waste: it displays one or more of the hazardous properties of Annex III to Directive 2008/98/EC (glossary). Weight of the waste in its original state, with no further manipulation such as a dry weight (E5 AR 5). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Waste register, waste transfer notes, service provider reports. | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-INCINERATION` | Déchets incinérés (élimination) / Waste incinerated (disposal) | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Waste of the site sent to incineration that is not a recovery operation, during the period, in tonnes (E5-5 §16(d)(i)). Incineration with energy recovery that meets condition R1 of Annex II to Directive 2008/98/EC is counted in other recovery operations (E5 AR 6). Weight of the waste in its original state, with no further manipulation such as a dry weight (E5 AR 5). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Waste register, waste transfer notes, service provider reports. | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-DECHARGE` | Déchets mis en décharge / Waste sent to landfill | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Waste of the site sent to landfill (a waste disposal site for deposit onto or into land) during the period, in tonnes (E5-5 §16(d)(ii)). Weight of the waste in its original state, with no further manipulation such as a dry weight (E5 AR 5). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Waste register, waste transfer notes, service provider reports. | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-AUTRE-ELIM` | Déchets envoyés vers d'autres opérations d'élimination / Waste sent to other disposal operations | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Waste of the site disposed of otherwise than by incineration or landfill, during the period, in tonnes (E5-5 §16(d)(iii), E5 AR 7). Disposal operations: Annex I to Directive 2008/98/EC on waste. Weight of the waste in its original state, with no further manipulation such as a dry weight (E5 AR 5). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Waste register, waste transfer notes, service provider reports. | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-ELIMINES` | Déchets dirigés vers l'élimination / Waste directed to disposal | t | Calculated | Sum / — | `[DEC-INCINERATION] + [DEC-DECHARGE] + [DEC-AUTRE-ELIM]` | Calculated by the tool: waste incinerated (disposal), sent to landfill and sent to other disposal operations (E5-5 §16(d)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Weight of the waste in its original state, with no further manipulation such as a dry weight (E5 AR 5). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Waste register, waste transfer notes, service provider reports. | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-RADIOACTIFS` | Déchets radioactifs générés / Radioactive waste generated | t | Input | Sum / sum | — | Total weight of the radioactive waste generated by the site during the period, in tonnes (E5-5 §17). Radioactive waste: as defined in Article 3(7) of Directive 2011/70/Euratom. Weight of the waste in its original state, with no further manipulation such as a dry weight (E5 AR 5). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | `DEC-PART-DETOURNES` | Part des déchets détournés de l'élimination (réemploi, recyclage, autres valorisations) / Share of waste diverted from disposal (reuse, recycling, other recovery) | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[DEC-DETOURNES] / [DEC-TOTAL]` | Calculated by the tool: waste diverted from disposal divided by the total waste generated (E5-5 §16(c)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-PART-DET-DANG` | Part des déchets détournés de l'élimination : dangereux / Share of waste diverted from disposal: hazardous | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[DEC-DETOURNES-DANG] / [DEC-TOTAL]` | Calculated by the tool: hazardous waste diverted from disposal divided by the total waste generated (E5-5 §16(c)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS E5 – Resource use and circular economy |
| `DEC-PART-DET-NON-DANG` | Part des déchets détournés de l'élimination : non dangereux / Share of waste diverted from disposal: non-hazardous | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `([DEC-DETOURNES] - [DEC-DETOURNES-DANG]) / [DEC-TOTAL]` | Calculated by the tool: non-hazardous waste diverted from disposal (diverted minus hazardous diverted) divided by the total waste generated (E5-5 §16(c)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Answer N/A if no policy refers to one. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

If not, answer No (ESRS 2 §39). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Whether a grievance mechanism exists is answered separately. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: HR information system, payroll. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-ROTATION-CDI` | Taux de rotation des salariés en contrat permanent / Turnover rate of permanent employees | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-DEPARTS-CDI] / [SOC-EFF-MOYEN]` | Calculated by the tool: leavers on permanent contracts (resignation, dismissal, retirement, death in service) divided by the average headcount of all employees, as AR 11 requires (ESRS S1, §20(d), AR 11). Recalculated at each level from the totals. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-EFF-ECART-COMPTES` | Écart entre l'effectif déclaré et les états financiers / Difference between the reported headcount and the financial statements | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the total headcount reported does not match the most representative number in the financial statements (for example an average or full-time equivalent figure): explanation of the difference (ESRS S1, §20(e)). Answer N/A otherwise. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-NON-SALARIES` | Non-salariés du personnel en fin de période / Non-employees in the own workforce at end of period | people | Input | Sum / last value | — | People in the company's own workforce who are not its employees, at the end of the period, as a headcount: self-employed people who supply labour to the company under contract, and people provided by employment agencies (NACE N78) (ESRS S1, §7, §22, AR 13). All self-employed people who supply labour are counted, not only those who work exclusively for the company. S1-6 is disclosed only if non-employees are connected to material impacts, risks or opportunities (ESRS S1, §1(b), AR 12); the number is also used for the share of the own workforce covered by health and safety (§37(a)): enter it as soon as it is known. An N/A answer (phase-in, non-employees not tracked) makes this share and the accident rate of the own workforce cover employees only. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in (ESRS 1 §125(f) to §127(f)): may be omitted for financial years before 2027 (wave-one undertakings) or for the first year of reporting (other undertakings). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Contracts of self-employed people, statements and invoices of employment agencies. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PART-CONVENTION` | Part des salariés couverts par une convention collective / Share of employees covered by collective bargaining agreements | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-SAL-CONVENTION] / [SOC-EFFECTIF]` | Calculated by the tool: employees covered by collective bargaining agreements divided by the total headcount (ESRS S1, §24(a), AR 14). Read by country (countries of the European Economic Area with significant employment, §24(b)) or by region outside the Area (§24(c)) in the views by organisation. Every site enters the number of its covered employees, outside the EEA too during the phase-in: an N/A answer would take its covered employees out of the calculation but not its headcount, and the share would be wrong; the EEA share is then read on the EEA organisations. Phase-in for employees outside the European Economic Area (ESRS 1 §125(f) to §127(f)): they may be left out for financial years before 2027 (wave-one undertakings) or for the first year of reporting (other undertakings). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

If yes, the share of employees covered by country is read in the views by organisation (ESRS S1, §24(b)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: HR information system, payroll. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PART-REPRESENTES` | Part des salariés couverts par des représentants des travailleurs / Share of employees covered by workers' representatives | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-SAL-REPRESENTES] / [SOC-EFFECTIF]` | Calculated by the tool: employees in establishments with workers' representatives divided by the total headcount (ESRS S1, §25(a), AR 16). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: HR information system, payroll. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-DIRECTION-AUTRE` | Membres de la direction générale d'un autre genre ou au genre non communiqué / Top management members of another gender or with gender not reported | people | Input | Sum / last value | — | Top management members of another gender or whose gender is not reported, at the end of the period, as a headcount (ESRS S1, §27): they count in the total used for the shares. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: HR information system, payroll. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-DIRECTION-PART-F` | Part des femmes dans la direction générale / Share of women in top management | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-DIRECTION-F] / ([SOC-DIRECTION-F] + [SOC-DIRECTION-H] + [SOC-DIRECTION-AUTRE])` | Calculated by the tool: women in top management divided by all its members (ESRS S1, §27). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-DIRECTION-PART-H` | Part des hommes dans la direction générale / Share of men in top management | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-DIRECTION-H] / ([SOC-DIRECTION-F] + [SOC-DIRECTION-H] + [SOC-DIRECTION-AUTRE])` | Calculated by the tool: men in top management divided by all its members (ESRS S1, §27). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-SALAIRE-DECENT` | Tous les salariés perçoivent un salaire décent / All employees are paid an adequate wage | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Are all employees paid an adequate wage? Compare, country by country, the lowest wage (interns and apprentices excluded: basic wage plus fixed payments guaranteed to all) with a benchmark that cannot be lower, in the EU, than the wage set by collective bargaining or the statutory minimum wage (Directive (EU) 2022/2041) and, outside the EU, than the adequate minimum wage or a living wage estimate (ILO principles) (ESRS S1, §29, AR 18 to AR 20). An adequate wage is not necessarily the minimum wage. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-SALAIRE-DECENT-REF` | Références de salaire décent et pays concernés / Adequate wage benchmarks and their countries | written text | Input | None / none | — | Benchmark or benchmarks used to decide whether a wage is adequate, with the countries for which each one is used (ESRS S1, §29, AR 20). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Collective agreements, statutory minimum wages, living wage estimates. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-SAL-SOUS-DECENT` | Salariés qui ne perçoivent pas un salaire décent / Employees not paid an adequate wage | employees | Input | Sum / last value | — | Employees whose wage is below the adequate wage benchmark of their country, at the end of the period, as a headcount (ESRS S1, §29, AR 19). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Payroll. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PART-SOUS-DECENT` | Part des salariés qui ne perçoivent pas un salaire décent / Share of employees not paid an adequate wage | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-SAL-SOUS-DECENT] / [SOC-EFFECTIF]` | Calculated by the tool: employees below the adequate wage benchmark divided by the total headcount (ESRS S1, §29). The standard asks for it for each country concerned: read it by country in the views by organisation. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PROTECTION-MALADIE` | Pays sans protection sociale : maladie / Countries without social protection: sickness | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if employees lack social protection, through public programmes or benefits offered by the company: countries where loss of income due to sickness is not covered by the existing social protection (ESRS S1, §31(a)). Answer ‘None’ if all employees are covered. Phase-in (ESRS 1 §125(f) to §127(f)): may be omitted for financial years before 2027 (wave-one undertakings) or for the first year of reporting (other undertakings). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PROTECTION-CHOMAGE` | Pays sans protection sociale : chômage / Countries without social protection: unemployment | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if employees lack social protection, through public programmes or benefits offered by the company: countries where unemployment starting while working for the company is not covered by the existing social protection (ESRS S1, §31(b)). Answer ‘None’ if all employees are covered. Phase-in (ESRS 1 §125(f) to §127(f)): may be omitted for financial years before 2027 (wave-one undertakings) or for the first year of reporting (other undertakings). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PROTECTION-ACCIDENT` | Pays sans protection sociale : accident du travail et invalidité / Countries without social protection: employment injury and disability | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if employees lack social protection, through public programmes or benefits offered by the company: countries where employment injury and acquired disability is not covered by the existing social protection (ESRS S1, §31(c)). Answer ‘None’ if all employees are covered. Phase-in (ESRS 1 §125(f) to §127(f)): may be omitted for financial years before 2027 (wave-one undertakings) or for the first year of reporting (other undertakings). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PROTECTION-MATERNITE` | Pays sans protection sociale : congé de maternité / Countries without social protection: maternity leave | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if employees lack social protection, through public programmes or benefits offered by the company: countries where maternity leave is not covered by the existing social protection (ESRS S1, §31(d)). Answer ‘None’ if all employees are covered. | `SOC-SAL-ENTRETIENS` | Salariés ayant eu un entretien formel de performance et de carrière / Employees with a formal performance and career review | employees | Input | Sum / none | — | Employees who took part during the period in a formalised performance and career development review (at least once a year, on criteria known to the employee and the manager), each counted once (ESRS S1, §35(a), AR 22). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in (ESRS 1 §125(f) to §127(f)): may be omitted for financial years before 2027 (wave-one undertakings) or for the first year of reporting (other undertakings). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: HR information system, payroll. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PART-ENTRETIENS` | Part des salariés ayant eu un entretien de performance et de carrière / Share of employees with a performance and career review | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-SAL-ENTRETIENS] / [SOC-EFFECTIF]` | Calculated by the tool: employees with a review divided by the total headcount of S1-5 (ESRS S1, §35(a), AR 22). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-SST-COUVERTS` | Personnel couvert par le système de management de la santé et de la sécurité / Own workforce covered by the health and safety management system | people | Input | Sum / last value | — | People in the own workforce (employees and non-employees) covered at the end of the period by the company's occupational health and safety management system, based on legal requirements or recognised standards or guidelines, as a headcount (ESRS S1, §37(a), AR 24). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accident register, accident reports, health and safety department. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PERSONNEL` | Personnel de l'entreprise (salariés et non-salariés) / Own workforce (employees and non-employees) | people | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-EFFECTIF] + [SOC-NON-SALARIES]` | Calculated by the tool: employee headcount plus non-employees in the own workforce, at period end; an N/A number of non-employees counts as 0 (ESRS S1, §7, §37(a)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-PART-SST` | Part du personnel couverte par le système de santé et de sécurité / Share of the own workforce covered by the health and safety management system | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-SST-COUVERTS] / [SOC-PERSONNEL]` | Calculated by the tool: people covered divided by the own workforce (employees and non-employees), as a headcount (ESRS S1, §37(a), AR 24). If the number of non-employees is N/A, the share covers employees. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-DECES-MALADIE` | Décès dus à une maladie liée au travail (salariés) / Fatalities from work-related ill health (employees) | people | Input | Sum / sum | — | Employees who died during the period from recordable work-related ill health (illness caused or made worse by work), subject to legal restrictions (ESRS S1, §37(b)(ii)). They are part of the work-related fatalities of employees. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accident register, accident reports, health and safety department. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-DECES-NON-SAL` | Décès dus à un accident du travail (non-salariés) / Fatalities from work-related accidents (non-employees) | people | Input | Sum / sum | — | Non-employees in the own workforce who died during the period as a result of a recordable work-related accident, subject to legal restrictions (ESRS S1, §37(b)(i)). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in for non-employees (ESRS 1 §125(f) to §127(f)): may be omitted for financial years before 2027 (wave-one undertakings) or for the first year of reporting (other undertakings). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accident register, accident reports, health and safety department. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-DECES-SITE` | Décès dus à un accident du travail (autres travailleurs sur les sites) / Fatalities from work-related accidents (other workers on the sites) | people | Input | Sum / sum | — | Workers who are not part of the own workforce (subcontractors, for example) but work on the company's sites, who died during the period as a result of a recordable work-related accident, subject to legal restrictions (ESRS S1, §37(b)(i)). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accident register, accident reports, health and safety department. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-DECES-ACCIDENTS` | Décès dus à un accident du travail (personnel et travailleurs sur les sites) / Fatalities from work-related accidents (own workforce and workers on the sites) | people | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-DECES] - [SOC-DECES-MALADIE] + [SOC-DECES-NON-SAL] + [SOC-DECES-SITE]` | Calculated by the tool: work-related fatalities of employees minus those from ill health, plus the accident fatalities of non-employees and of other workers on the sites (ESRS S1, §37(b)(i)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-DECES-TOUS` | Décès liés au travail, tous travailleurs / Work-related fatalities, all workers | people | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-DECES] + [SOC-DECES-NON-SAL] + [SOC-DECES-SITE]` | Calculated by the tool: the sum the standard asks for, fatalities from work-related accidents (own workforce and other workers on the sites) and fatalities of employees from work-related ill health (ESRS S1, §37(b)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-ACCIDENTS-NON-SAL` | Accidents du travail comptabilisables (non-salariés) / Recordable work-related accidents (non-employees) | accidents | Input | Sum / sum | — | Work-related accidents of non-employees in the own workforce during the period that resulted in death or more than three days of absence (ESRS S1, §37(c), AR 25, AR 26; glossary). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in for non-employees (ESRS 1 §125(f) to §127(f)): may be omitted for financial years before 2027 (wave-one undertakings) or for the first year of reporting (other undertakings). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accident register, accident reports, health and safety department. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-ACCIDENTS-TOUS` | Accidents du travail comptabilisables (personnel) / Recordable work-related accidents (own workforce) | accidents | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-ACCIDENTS-ENREG] + [SOC-ACCIDENTS-NON-SAL]` | Calculated by the tool: accidents of employees plus accidents of non-employees; fatal accidents are included (ESRS S1, §37(c), AR 26). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-HEURES-NON-SAL` | Heures travaillées par les non-salariés / Hours worked by non-employees | h | Input | Sum / sum | — | Hours worked by non-employees in the own workforce during the period; if not known, estimated from normal working hours, paid leave deducted (ESRS S1, §37(c), AR 25). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in for non-employees (ESRS 1 §125(f) to §127(f)): may be omitted for financial years before 2027 (wave-one undertakings) or for the first year of reporting (other undertakings). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Contracts of self-employed people, statements and invoices of employment agencies. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-HEURES-PERSONNEL` | Heures travaillées par le personnel (salariés et non-salariés) / Hours worked by the own workforce (employees and non-employees) | h | Calculated | Sum / — | `[SOC-HEURES-TRAV] + [SOC-HEURES-NON-SAL]` | Calculated by the tool: hours worked by employees plus those of non-employees; N/A hours of non-employees count as 0 (ESRS S1, §37(c), AR 25). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-TAUX-ACC-SALARIES` | Taux d'accidents du travail comptabilisables des salariés, par million d'heures travaillées / Rate of recordable work-related accidents of employees, per million hours worked | — | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-ACCIDENTS-ENREG] * 1000000 / [SOC-HEURES-TRAV]` | Calculated by the tool: recordable accidents of employees per million hours worked by employees (ESRS S1, §37(c), AR 25). Recalculated at each level from the totals. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-TAUX-ACC-NON-SAL` | Taux d'accidents du travail comptabilisables des non-salariés, par million d'heures travaillées / Rate of recordable work-related accidents of non-employees, per million hours worked | — | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-ACCIDENTS-NON-SAL] * 1000000 / [SOC-HEURES-NON-SAL]` | Calculated by the tool: recordable accidents of non-employees per million hours worked by non-employees (ESRS S1, §37(c), AR 25). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-TAUX-ACCIDENTS` | Taux d'accidents du travail comptabilisables du personnel, par million d'heures travaillées / Rate of recordable work-related accidents of the own workforce, per million hours worked | — | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-ACCIDENTS-TOUS] * 1000000 / [SOC-HEURES-PERSONNEL]` | Calculated by the tool: recordable accidents of the own workforce (employees and non-employees) divided by the total hours worked by the own workforce, multiplied by 1,000,000: accidents per million hours, about 500 full-time people over one year (ESRS S1, §37(c), AR 25). Recalculated at each level from the totals. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |

| `SOC-PART-CONGES-FAMILLE` | Part des salariés ayant droit à des congés familiaux / Share of employees entitled to family-related leave | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-SAL-CONGES-FAMILLE] / [SOC-EFFECTIF]` | Calculated by the tool: employees entitled to family-related leave divided by the total headcount (ESRS S1, §39). | `SOC-S1-REMU-H-HOMMES` | Rémunération horaire brute moyenne de tous les salariés hommes / Average gross hourly pay level of all male employees | €/h | Input | None / last value | — | Average gross hourly pay level of all male employees of the company (ESRS S1, §41(a), AR 31): ordinary basic salary and any other remuneration made available to all employees, in cash or in kind, received directly or indirectly from the employer in respect of the employment (complementary or variable components) (AR 32). Method of ESRS S1, entered once for the whole company; say in the methods text how the average is calculated (AR 34). The Voluntary Standard VS and pay transparency have their own KPIs and their own calculation. Restricted data. Amount in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50): the unit follows the reference currency of the space (Frameworks › Units and formats), to set before you install the template; no value is converted. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)).
