Method of ESRS S1, entered once for the whole company; say in the methods text how the average is calculated (AR 34). The Voluntary Standard VS and pay transparency have their own KPIs and their own calculation. Restricted data. Amount in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50): the unit follows the reference currency of the space (Frameworks › Units and formats), to set before you install the template; no value is converted. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Payroll. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-ECART-REMU-H` | Écart de rémunération entre femmes et hommes (horaire, tous les salariés) / Gender pay gap (hourly, all employees) | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `([SOC-S1-REMU-H-HOMMES] - [SOC-S1-REMU-H-FEMMES]) / [SOC-S1-REMU-H-HOMMES]` | Calculated by the tool, never entered: difference between the average gross hourly pay of men and that of women, as a share of men's, over all the company's employees; positive when women are paid less (ESRS S1, §41(a), AR 31 to AR 33). Unadjusted gap. The average hourly pay of men and of women is entered for the whole company: the gap is calculated at that level. Method of ESRS S1, on its own hourly pay figures: neither the gap of the Voluntary Standard VS (B10 §42(b), calculated in EFRAG's workbook) nor the per-employer gap of pay transparency. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-REMU-PLUS-HAUTE` | Rémunération totale annuelle de la personne la mieux payée / Annual total remuneration of the highest-paid individual | € | Input | None / last value | — | Annual total remuneration of the company's highest-paid individual: base salary, cash benefits (bonuses, commissions, profit-sharing), benefits in kind (car, health insurance…) and fair value of the year's long-term incentives (stock options, performance shares…) (ESRS S1, §41(b), AR 35). Confidential data: it points to one person. Amount in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50): the unit follows the reference currency of the space (Frameworks › Units and formats), to set before you install the template; no value is converted. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Payroll. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-REMU-MEDIANE` | Rémunération totale annuelle médiane des salariés (hors la mieux payée) / Median annual total remuneration of employees (excluding the highest-paid) | € | Input | None / last value | — | Median annual total remuneration of all employees, without the highest-paid individual, with the same pay components: half of the employees earn more, half earn less (ESRS S1, §41(b), AR 35). A median cannot be derived from those of the entities: entered for the whole company. Amount in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50): the unit follows the reference currency of the space (Frameworks › Units and formats), to set before you install the template; no value is converted. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Payroll. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-RATIO-REMU` | Ratio de la rémunération totale annuelle / Annual total remuneration ratio | — | Calculated | Formula / — | `[SOC-REMU-PLUS-HAUTE] / [SOC-REMU-MEDIANE]` | Calculated by the tool: annual total remuneration of the highest-paid individual divided by the median of the other employees; a number of times, without unit (ESRS S1, §41(b), AR 35). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-DISCRIMINATIONS` | Incidents de discrimination avérés / Substantiated incidents of discrimination | cases | Input | Sum / sum | — | Incidents of discrimination at work (gender, racial or ethnic origin, nationality, religion or belief, disability, age, sexual orientation or other, harassment included) substantiated and identified during the period: judicial or non-judicial proceedings and other incidents, evidenced by verifiable facts and formally found by a court, the OECD or an administrative authority, subject to privacy rules (ESRS S1, §43(a), AR 37). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Legal department, human resources, grievance mechanism. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-INCIDENTS-DH` | Incidents avérés touchant les droits humains du personnel (hors discriminations) / Substantiated human rights incidents (own workforce, excluding discrimination) | cases | Input | Sum / sum | — | Substantiated incidents of not respecting internationally recognised human rights connected to the own workforce, identified during the period, excluding discrimination (counted separately): judicial or non-judicial proceedings and other incidents, substantiated and found as for discrimination (ESRS S1, §43(b), AR 36). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Legal department, human resources, grievance mechanism. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-AMENDES-DH` | Amendes, sanctions et indemnités pour ces incidents / Fines, penalties and compensation for these incidents | € | Input | Sum / sum | — | Total fines, penalties and compensation for damages imposed through judicial or non-judicial proceedings for incidents of discrimination or other human rights incidents, recognised in the financial statements of the period (ESRS S1, §43(c), AR 41). Amount in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50): the unit follows the reference currency of the space (Frameworks › Units and formats), to set before you install the template; no value is converted. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accounting, legal department. | ESRS S1 – Own workforce |
| `SOC-CHAINE-POLITIQUES` | Politiques adoptées sur les travailleurs de la chaîne de valeur / Policies adopted on workers in the value chain | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company adopted policies to manage its material impacts, risks and opportunities on workers in the value chain? (ESRS S2-2 §15). Describe the channels in the text « Channels for workers in the value chain to raise concerns ». | `SOC-COMMU-POLITIQUES` | Politiques adoptées sur les communautés affectées / Policies adopted on affected communities | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company adopted policies to manage its material impacts, risks and opportunities on affected communities? If not, answer No: the standard asks to say so (ESRS 2 §39). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-POLIT-CONTENU` | Politiques sur les communautés affectées : contenu et changements / Policies on affected communities: contents and changes | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each policy: its key contents, its general objectives and the material impacts, risks or opportunities it addresses; describe the changes made during the period (ESRS 2 §42(a)). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-POLIT-PERIMETRE` | Politiques sur les communautés affectées : périmètre et exclusions / Policies on affected communities: scope and exclusions | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each policy: its scope or exclusions (own operations, upstream and downstream value chain, geographies and, if defined, the affected stakeholder groups) (ESRS 2 §42(b)). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-POLIT-REFERENCES` | Politiques sur les communautés affectées : normes ou initiatives de tiers / Policies on affected communities: third-party standards or initiatives | written text | Input | None / none | — | Third-party standards or initiatives that the company commits to respect through its policies (ESRS 2 §42(c)). Answer N/A if no policy refers to one. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-POLIT-PARTIES` | Politiques sur les communautés affectées : intérêts des parties prenantes concernées / Policies on affected communities: interests of affected stakeholders | written text | Input | None / none | — | How the interests of affected stakeholders were considered in setting each policy, if they were (ESRS 2 §42(d)). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-ACTIONS` | Actions clés sur les communautés affectées / Key actions on affected communities | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company taken or planned key actions to manage its material impacts, risks and opportunities on affected communities? If not, answer No (ESRS 2 §39). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-ACT-DESC` | Actions clés sur les communautés affectées : description, périmètre et résultats attendus / Key actions on affected communities: description, scope and expected outcomes | written text | Input | None / none | — | Key actions taken during the period and planned, with their timeframe, their scope (own operations, value chain, subsidiaries, geographies, stakeholders) and their expected outcomes, and how they serve the policy objectives (ESRS 2 §45). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-ACT-TAB` | Actions clés sur les communautés affectées : calendrier et ressources financières / Key actions on affected communities: timeframe and financial resources | table: Key action, Status, Timeframe, Scope, Capital expenditure of the period, Operating expenditure of the period, Future resources, low estimate, Future resources, high estimate, Depends on preconditions | Input | None / none | — | One row per key action: status, timeframe, scope, capital and operating expenditure of the period, range of future resources, and whether the action depends on preconditions (ESRS 2 §45-46). Amounts only when significant resources are allocated. Amounts in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). Source: Action plans, budget, accounting (capital and operating expenditure). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-ACT-MOYENS` | Actions clés sur les communautés affectées : nature des ressources, conditions et postes comptables / Key actions on affected communities: type of resources, preconditions and line items | written text | Input | None / none | — | When significant financial resources are allocated to the actions: their type (current and future), the preconditions the actions depend on (financial support, public policy, market) and the line items or notes of the financial statements that contain the amounts (ESRS 2 §46). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-CIBLES` | Cibles mesurables sur les communautés affectées / Measurable targets on affected communities | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company set measurable, time-bound and outcome-oriented targets on affected communities? If not, answer No and describe below how it tracks the effectiveness of its policies and actions (ESRS 2 §52). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-CIB-TAB` | Cibles sur les communautés affectées : valeurs, années et périmètre / Targets on affected communities: values, years and scope | table: Target, Metric, Absolute or relative, Unit, Base year, Baseline value, Milestone year, Milestone value, Target year, Target value, Required by law, Based on conclusive scientific evidence, Scope | Input | None / none | — | One row per target: metric and unit, absolute or relative target, baseline value and year, milestone, target value and year, whether it is required by law, whether it is based on conclusive scientific evidence (environmental topics), scope (ESRS 2 §51). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). Source: Company strategy, roadmaps and dashboards. | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-CIB-DESC` | Cibles sur les communautés affectées : lien avec les politiques, étapes, méthodes et scénarios / Targets on affected communities: link to policies, milestones, methods and scenarios | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each target: its link to the policy objectives and actions, the milestones or interim targets, the methods and significant assumptions and, where applicable, the scenarios, the key data sources and the compatibility with national, EU or international policy goals (ESRS 2 §51(a), (e), (f), (h)). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-SUIVI-SS-CIBLE` | Suivi de l'efficacité sans cible sur les communautés affectées / Tracking of effectiveness without targets on affected communities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if no measurable target is set: whether and how the company still tracks the effectiveness of its policies and actions (ESRS 2 §52). Answer N/A if targets are set. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-POLIT-COUVERTURE` | Politiques : toutes les communautés affectées ou des communautés précises / Policies: all affected communities or specific communities | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each policy: does it cover all affected communities or only specific communities (for example a community of indigenous peoples or a community living around a site)? Name the groups concerned (ESRS S3-1 §8). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-DIALOGUE` | Dialogue avec les communautés affectées / Engagement with affected communities | written text | Input | None / none | — | How the company engages directly with affected communities, their legitimate representatives or credible proxies, and how their perspectives inform its decisions and activities to manage actual and potential impacts during the year (ESRS S3-2 §11). Engagement can take different forms, such as information sessions, consultation or participation (ESRS S3 AR 2). Only if the company engages directly with them (see the previous yes / no item); answer N/A otherwise. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-VULNERABLES` | Points de vue des communautés affectées vulnérables / Perspectives of vulnerable affected communities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company takes action to understand the perspectives of affected communities who may be particularly vulnerable or marginalised (for example women, girls, migrants, persons with disabilities): how it gains insight into them (ESRS S3-2 §11). Answer N/A if it takes no such action. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-CANAUX` | Canaux ouverts aux communautés affectées pour faire part de leurs préoccupations / Channels for affected communities to raise concerns | written text | Input | None / none | — | Channels available to affected communities to bring their concerns or needs directly to the company and have them addressed, and how the company assesses their effectiveness (ESRS S3-2 §13). The effectiveness criteria for non-judicial grievance mechanisms of principle 31 of the UN Guiding Principles can be used (ESRS S3 AR 4). If the company protects the people who use these channels against retaliation and has disclosed it under business conduct (ESRS G1-1), it can refer to that disclosure. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-PLAINTES` | Mécanisme de traitement des plaintes pour les communautés affectées / Grievance mechanism for affected communities | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company put in place a grievance mechanism that affected communities can use? (ESRS S3-2 §13). Describe the channels in the text « Channels for affected communities to raise concerns ». Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-REPARATION` | Approche de la réparation pour les communautés affectées / Approach to remediation for affected communities | written text | Input | None / none | — | General approach and processes to provide or cooperate in remediation where the company has caused or contributed to a material negative impact on affected communities (ESRS S3-2 §14). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-ACTIONS-IMPACTS` | Actions contre les impacts négatifs importants sur les communautés affectées / Actions on material negative impacts on affected communities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Key actions taken, planned or under way to prevent, mitigate, bring to an end, minimise and remediate material negative impacts on affected communities, including the approach when these actions are in tension with other business pressures (for example practices in planning and land acquisition, or in the exploitation and financing of raw materials) (ESRS S3-3 §16(a)). Leverage and collective actions (multi-stakeholder or industry initiatives) are included (ESRS S3 AR 5). Present the measures against the effects of the transition to a low-carbon economy in a way that shows the link between topics (ESRS S3 AR 6). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-ACTIONS-SUIVI` | Suivi de l'efficacité des actions sur les communautés affectées / Effectiveness tracking of actions on affected communities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if actions on material negative impacts are reported: how the company tracks and assesses the effectiveness of these actions in delivering outcomes for the people concerned (ESRS S3-3 §16(b)). If it explains this in the targets (ESRS 2 GDR-T) or in an entity-specific metric (ESRS 2 GDR-M), a reference is enough. Answer N/A if no action on negative impacts is reported. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-INCIDENTS-DH` | Incidents liés aux droits humains concernant les communautés affectées / Human rights incidents connected to affected communities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Human rights incidents connected to affected communities identified during the period (ESRS S3-3 §17), subject to the relevant privacy rules. Report only substantiated and verified instances: backed by objective, factual information, and found by a court, the OECD or an administrative authority (ESRS S3 AR 7). This covers judicial and non-judicial proceedings and other incidents the company registered. You may group the cases by type of incident or by people concerned (ESRS S3 AR 10). Consider any legal disputes on land rights and on the free, prior and informed consent of indigenous peoples (S3 AR 8). Write that there were none, if that is the case. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-INDICATEURS` | Indicateurs propres à l'entreprise sur les communautés affectées / Entity-specific metrics on affected communities | table: Metric, Value, Unit, Scope | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company tracks this topic with its own metrics (the standard prescribes none): one row per metric, with its value, unit and scope (ESRS 2 §48, AR 42). Leave empty otherwise. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). Source: Company strategy, roadmaps and dashboards. | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-METHODES` | Indicateurs sur les communautés affectées : méthodes, sources, chaîne de valeur et contexte / Metrics on affected communities: methods, sources, value chain data and context | written text | Input | None / none | — | For each metric: unit, calculation method and sources, estimation method with its significant assumptions and limitations; for a value chain metric, reliance on indirect sources or proxies and the planned improvements; contextual information (ESRS 2 §49(a) to (c)). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-EVOLUTION` | Indicateurs sur les communautés affectées : évolutions importantes et progrès / Metrics on affected communities: significant changes and progress | written text | Input | None / none | — | Significant changes in performance compared with the previous periods, progress towards the targets and effect of major acquisitions or disposals (ESRS 2 §49(d)). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-DIALOGUE-DIRECT` | Dialogue direct avec les communautés affectées / Direct engagement with affected communities | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Does the company engage directly with affected communities, their legitimate representatives or credible proxies? If yes, explain how in the text « Engagement with affected communities » (ESRS S3-2 §11). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-POLIT-AUTOCHTONES` | Politiques sur les impacts sur les peuples autochtones / Policies on impacts on indigenous peoples | written text | Input | None / none | — | Particular policy provisions for preventing and addressing impacts on indigenous peoples (ESRS S3-1 §9). Write that there are none, if that is the case. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-AUTOCHTONES-CLPE` | Droits des peuples autochtones, dont le consentement libre, préalable et éclairé / Rights of indigenous peoples, including free, prior and informed consent | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if affected communities are indigenous peoples: how the company takes into account and ensures respect of their particular rights in its engagement, including the right to free, prior and informed consent for their cultural, intellectual, religious and spiritual property, for activities affecting their lands and territories, and for legislative or administrative measures that affect them (ESRS S3-2 §12). For example: the process to obtain their consent and to ensure good-faith negotiations when relocation or occupation occurs (S3 AR 3). Answer N/A if no affected community is an indigenous people. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-AUTOCHTONES-CONS` | Peuples autochtones consultés sur le dialogue / Indigenous peoples consulted on the engagement | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Only if the company engages with indigenous peoples: have they been consulted on the mode and parameters of the engagement (for example the agenda, nature and timing)? (ESRS S3-2 §12). Answer N/A if there is no engagement with indigenous peoples. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |
| `SOC-COMMU-AUTOCHT-CONS-DESC` | Consultation des peuples autochtones sur le dialogue / How indigenous peoples were consulted on the engagement | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company engages with indigenous peoples: how they have been consulted on the mode and parameters of the engagement (ESRS S3-2 §12). Answer N/A if there is no engagement with indigenous peoples. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S3 – Affected communities |

Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |

Answer N/A if targets are set. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |

Name the groups concerned (ESRS S4-1 §9). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |
| `SOC-CONSO-DIALOGUE` | Dialogue avec les consommateurs et utilisateurs finaux / Engagement with consumers and end-users | written text | Input | None / none | — | How the company engages directly with consumers and end-users, their legitimate representatives or credible proxies, and how their perspectives inform its decisions and activities to manage actual and potential impacts during the year (ESRS S4-2 §11). Engagement can take different forms, such as information sessions, consultation or participation (ESRS S4 AR 2). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |
| `SOC-CONSO-VULNERABLES` | Points de vue des consommateurs et utilisateurs finaux vulnérables / Perspectives of vulnerable consumers and end-users | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if the company takes action to understand the perspectives of consumers and end-users who may be particularly vulnerable or marginalised (for example persons with disabilities, children): how it gains insight into them (ESRS S4-2 §11). Answer N/A if it takes no such action. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |
| `SOC-CONSO-CANAUX` | Canaux ouverts aux consommateurs et utilisateurs finaux pour faire part de leurs préoccupations / Channels for consumers and end-users to raise concerns | written text | Input | None / none | — | Channels available to consumers and end-users to bring their concerns or needs directly to the company and have them addressed, and how the company assesses their effectiveness (ESRS S4-2 §12). The effectiveness criteria for non-judicial grievance mechanisms of principle 31 of the UN Guiding Principles can be used (ESRS S4 AR 3). If the company protects the people who use these channels against retaliation and has disclosed it under business conduct (ESRS G1-1), it can refer to that disclosure. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |
| `SOC-CONSO-PLAINTES` | Mécanisme de traitement des plaintes pour les consommateurs et utilisateurs finaux / Grievance mechanism for consumers and end-users | yes / no | Input | Count / last value | — | Has the company put in place a grievance mechanism that consumers and end-users can use? (ESRS S4-2 §12). Describe the channels in the text « Channels for consumers and end-users to raise concerns ». Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |
| `SOC-CONSO-REPARATION` | Approche de la réparation pour les consommateurs et utilisateurs finaux / Approach to remediation for consumers and end-users | written text | Input | None / none | — | General approach and processes to provide or cooperate in remediation where the company has caused or contributed to a material negative impact on consumers and end-users (ESRS S4-2 §13). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |
| `SOC-CONSO-ACTIONS-IMPACTS` | Actions contre les impacts négatifs importants sur les consommateurs et utilisateurs finaux / Actions on material negative impacts on consumers and end-users | written text | Input | None / none | — | Key actions taken, planned or under way to prevent, mitigate, bring to an end, minimise and remediate material negative impacts on consumers and end-users, including the approach when these actions are in tension with other business pressures (for example practices related to marketing, sales and data use) (ESRS S4-3 §15(a)). Leverage and collective actions (multi-stakeholder or industry initiatives) are included (ESRS S4 AR 4). Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |
| `SOC-CONSO-ACTIONS-SUIVI` | Suivi de l'efficacité des actions sur les consommateurs et utilisateurs finaux / Effectiveness tracking of actions on consumers and end-users | written text | Input | None / none | — | Only if actions on material negative impacts are reported: how the company tracks and assesses the effectiveness of these actions in delivering outcomes for the people concerned (ESRS S4-3 §15(b)). If it explains this in the targets (ESRS 2 GDR-T) or in an entity-specific metric (ESRS 2 GDR-M), a reference is enough. Answer N/A if no action on negative impacts is reported. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |
| `SOC-CONSO-INCIDENTS-DH` | Incidents liés aux droits humains concernant les consommateurs et utilisateurs finaux / Human rights incidents connected to consumers and end-users | written text | Input | None / none | — | Human rights incidents connected to consumers and end-users identified during the period (ESRS S4-3 §16), subject to the relevant privacy rules. Report only substantiated and verified instances: backed by objective, factual information, and found by a court, the OECD or an administrative authority (ESRS S4 AR 5). This covers judicial and non-judicial proceedings and other incidents the company registered. You may group the cases by type of incident or by people concerned (ESRS S4 AR 7). Write that there were none, if that is the case. Phase-in: wave-one companies may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2), other companies for their first two financial years of reporting (ESRS 1 §125(a), §126(a) and §127(a)). | ESRS S4 – Consumers and end-users |

Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-CONTRIBUTIONS-POLIT` | Contributions politiques financières et en nature / Financial and in-kind political contributions | € | Input | Sum / sum | — | Total monetary value of financial and in-kind political contributions made during the period, directly and indirectly (ESRS G1-5 §14). Political contribution: financial or in-kind support given directly to political parties, their elected representatives or persons seeking political office (donations, loans, sponsorships, advance payments, tickets to fundraising events; advertising, use of facilities, printing, equipment, board membership or employment) (G1 AR 7). Indirect: made through an intermediary (lobbyist, charity) or support to a think tank or trade association linked to a party or cause (G1 AR 8). Value in-kind contributions at their monetary value. The breakdown by country comes from the organisation tree. Amount in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50): the unit follows the reference currency of the space (Frameworks › Units and formats), to set before you install the template; no value is converted. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accounting, legal department. | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-CONTRIBUTIONS-DETAIL` | Contributions politiques par type de bénéficiaire / Political contributions by type of recipient | written text | Input | None / none | — | Breakdown of the political contributions by type of recipient or beneficiary (party, elected representative, candidate; for indirect contributions, intermediary, think tank, trade association) (ESRS G1-5 §14, G1 AR 7 and 8). Write the amounts. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-LOBBYING` | Sujets et positions de l'activité de lobbying / Main topics and positions of lobbying activities | written text | Input | None / none | — | Main issues covered by the company's lobbying activities and main positions taken, with an explanation of how these activities interact with its material impacts, risks and opportunities (ESRS G1-5 §15). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-NOMINATIONS-PUBLIC` | Membres des organes issus de l'administration publique / Members of the bodies appointed from public administration | written text | Input | None / none | — | Appointments during the period of members of the administrative, management and supervisory bodies who held a comparable position in public administration (regulators included) in the two preceding years. To judge whether a position is comparable, consider the level of responsibility and the scope of the activities (ESRS G1-5 §16, G1 AR 9). Write that there were none, if that is the case. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-PAIEMENT-CONDITIONS` | Conditions de paiement standard par catégorie de fournisseurs / Standard payment terms by main category of suppliers | written text | Input | None / none | — | Description of the company's standard payment terms, in number of days, by main category of suppliers; specify those that apply to small and medium-sized undertakings (SMEs) if they differ (ESRS G1-6 §18(a)). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-PAIEMENT-JOURS` | Conditions de paiement standard, en jours / Standard payment terms, in days | days | Input | None / last value | — | Number of days of the standard payment terms applied to suppliers (ESRS G1-6 §18(a)). If the terms differ by category, enter those of the main category and describe the others in the text « Standard payment terms by main category of suppliers ». It is not the actual average payment time. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: General terms of purchase, payment policy, accounts payable. | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-PAIEMENT-JOURS-PME` | Conditions de paiement standard des PME, en jours / Standard payment terms for SME suppliers, in days | days | Input | None / last value | — | Only if the standard payment terms of SME suppliers differ from those of other suppliers: number of days (ESRS G1-6 §18(a)). Answer N/A if they are the same. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: General terms of purchase, payment policy, accounts payable. | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-FACTURES` | Factures fournisseurs payées / Supplier invoices paid | invoices | Input | Sum / sum | — | Number of supplier invoices paid during the period. Together with « Invoices paid within the standard terms », it gives the tool the share of payments aligned with the standard terms (ESRS G1-6 §18(b)). The standard does not say whether the share is based on the number or on the value of the payments; the tool gives both: by number of invoices here, by value with the amounts paid. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accounts payable: list of the invoices paid in the period. | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-FACTURES-A-TEMPS` | Factures payées dans les délais / Invoices paid within the standard terms | invoices | Input | Sum / sum | — | Among the invoices paid during the period, the number paid within the company's standard payment terms (ESRS G1-6 §18(b)). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accounts payable: due date and payment date of each invoice. | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-PAIEMENT-PART-CONFORME` | Part des paiements conformes aux conditions standard, en nombre de factures / Share of payments aligned with the standard terms, by number of invoices | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[GOV-FACTURES-A-TEMPS] / [GOV-FACTURES]` | Calculated by the tool: invoices paid within the standard terms divided by invoices paid (ESRS G1-6 §18(b), G1 AR 10). If late payment to SMEs is a material topic for the company, add an entity-specific metric (ESRS 1 §11). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-FACTURES-MONTANT` | Montant des factures fournisseurs payées / Amount of supplier invoices paid | € | Input | Sum / sum | — | Total amount of the supplier invoices paid during the period. Together with « Amount of invoices paid within the standard terms », it gives the tool the share of payments aligned with the standard terms by value (ESRS G1-6 §18(b)). Amount in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50): the unit follows the reference currency of the space (Frameworks › Units and formats), to set before you install the template; no value is converted. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accounts payable: amounts of the invoices paid in the period. | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-FACTURES-MONTANT-A-TEMPS` | Montant des factures payées dans les délais / Amount of invoices paid within the standard terms | € | Input | Sum / sum | — | Among the invoices paid during the period, the amount of those paid within the company's standard payment terms (ESRS G1-6 §18(b)). Amount in the currency of the financial statements (ESRS 1 AR 50): the unit follows the reference currency of the space (Frameworks › Units and formats), to set before you install the template; no value is converted. Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Accounts payable: due date, payment date and amount of each payment. | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-PAIEMENT-PART-CONFORME-VAL` | Part des paiements conformes aux conditions standard, en valeur / Share of payments aligned with the standard terms, by value | % | Calculated | Formula / — | `[GOV-FACTURES-MONTANT-A-TEMPS] / [GOV-FACTURES-MONTANT]` | Calculated by the tool: amount of the invoices paid within the standard terms divided by the amount of the invoices paid (ESRS G1-6 §18(b)). The standard does not say whether the share is measured by number or by value: the tool gives both, the company says in its methods which one it discloses, or discloses both. Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |
| `GOV-PAIEMENT-CONTENTIEUX` | Procédures judiciaires en cours pour retard de paiement / Outstanding legal proceedings for late payment | cases | Input | Sum / last value | — | Number of legal proceedings outstanding at the end of the period for late payments (ESRS G1-6 §18(c)). Enter 0 if there is none (N/A would make the total not applicable). Phase-in: wave-one companies that, at the balance sheet date, do not exceed a net turnover of EUR 450 million or an average of 1,000 employees during the financial year may omit it for financial years before 2027, subject to the disclosures of ESRS 2 §7 to 10 (BP-2) (ESRS 1 §126(a)). Source: Legal department, compliance, register of court decisions. | ESRS G1 – Business conduct |

## Consistency rules

Checked at the entry level of the left-hand KPI.

| Name | Rule |
|---|---|
| Renewable electricity purchased ≤ electricity purchased | `[NRJ-ELEC-REN] <= [NRJ-ELEC]` |
| Female headcount ≤ total headcount | `[SOC-EFF-FEMMES] <= [SOC-EFFECTIF]` |
| Employees on permanent contracts ≤ total headcount | `[SOC-EFF-CDI] <= [SOC-EFFECTIF]` |
| Employees with disabilities ≤ total headcount | `[SOC-HANDICAP] <= [SOC-EFFECTIF]` |
| Hazardous waste ≤ waste generated | `[DEC-DANGEREUX] <= [DEC-TOTAL]` |
| Male headcount ≤ total headcount | `[SOC-EFF-HOMMES] <= [SOC-EFFECTIF]` |
| Employees covered by agreements ≤ total headcount | `[SOC-SAL-CONVENTION] <= [SOC-EFFECTIF]` |
| Recycled waste ≤ waste generated | `[DEC-RECYCLES] <= [DEC-TOTAL]` |
| Waste prepared for reuse ≤ waste generated | `[DEC-REEMPLOI] <= [DEC-TOTAL]` |
| Water discharged by processes ≤ water withdrawn | `[EAU-REJET-PROCESS] <= [EAU-PRELEVEMENT]` |
| Women with variable pay ≤ women included | `[SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-F] <= [SOC-REMU-NB-F]` |
| Men with variable pay ≤ men included | `[SOC-REMU-VAR-NB-H] <= [SOC-REMU-NB-H]` |
| Purchased nuclear and renewable energy ≤ purchased energy | `[NRJ-ACHAT-NUCLEAIRE] + [NRJ-ACHAT-RENOUV] <= ([NRJ-ELEC] + [NRJ-CHALEUR] + [NRJ-FROID]) / 1000` |
| Coal and other petroleum products ≤ other fossil fuels | `[NRJ-CHARBON] + [NRJ-PETROLE-AUTRES] <= [NRJ-COMB-AUTRES]` |
| Renewable energy production ≥ self-generated renewable electricity consumed | `[NRJ-PROD-REN] >= [NRJ-ELEC-AUTO] / 1000` |
| Scope 1 covered by the EU ETS ≤ gross scope 1 | `[GES-S1-SEQE] <= [GES-S1-BRUT]` |
| Scope 1 outside the consolidated group ≤ gross scope 1 | `[GES-S1-HORS-GROUPE] <= [GES-S1-BRUT]` |
| Location-based scope 2 outside the consolidated group ≤ location-based scope 2 | `[GES-S2-LB-HORS-GROUPE] <= [GES-S2-LB]` |
| Market-based scope 2 outside the consolidated group ≤ market-based scope 2 | `[GES-S2-MB-HORS-GROUPE] <= [GES-S2-MB]` |
| Cloud computing ≤ scope 3 category 1 | `[GES-S3-CLOUD] <= [GES-S3-ACHATS]` |
| SVHC procured ≤ substances of concern procured | `[POL-SVHC-ACHETEES] <= [POL-SOC-ACHETEES]` |
| SVHC manufactured ≤ substances of concern manufactured | `[POL-SVHC-FABRIQUEES] <= [POL-SOC-FABRIQUEES]` |
| SVHC placed on the market ≤ substances of concern placed on the market | `[POL-SVHC-MISES-MARCHE] <= [POL-SOC-MISES-MARCHE]` |
| SVHC released ≤ substances of concern released | `[POL-SVHC-REJETEES] <= [POL-SOC-REJETEES]` |
| Consumption in water-stress areas ≤ total consumption | `[EAU-CONSO-STRESS] <= [EAU-CONSOMMATION]` |
| Waste diverted + disposed of + unknown destination ≤ waste generated | `[DEC-DETOURNES] + [DEC-ELIMINES] + [DEC-INCONNUE] <= [DEC-TOTAL]` |
| Hazardous waste diverted ≤ waste diverted | `[DEC-DETOURNES-DANG] <= [DEC-DETOURNES]` |
| Hazardous waste directed to disposal ≤ waste directed to disposal | `[DEC-ELIMINES-DANG] <= [DEC-ELIMINES]` |
| Hazardous waste diverted + disposed of ≤ hazardous waste generated | `[DEC-DETOURNES-DANG] + [DEC-ELIMINES-DANG] <= [DEC-DANGEREUX]` |
| Secondary resources ≤ weight of key materials | `[ECO-CIRC-SECONDAIRES] <= [ECO-CIRC-MATIERES-POIDS]` |
| Permanent women and men ≤ permanent employees | `[SOC-EFF-CDI-F] + [SOC-EFF-CDI-H] <= [SOC-EFF-CDI]` |
| Temporary women and men ≤ temporary employees | `[SOC-EFF-TEMP-F] + [SOC-EFF-TEMP-H] <= [SOC-EFF-TEMPORAIRES]` |
| Permanent and temporary employees ≤ total headcount | `[SOC-EFF-CDI] + [SOC-EFF-TEMPORAIRES] <= [SOC-EFFECTIF]` |
| Non-guaranteed hours employees ≤ total headcount | `[SOC-EFF-HEURES-NON-GARANTIES] <= [SOC-EFFECTIF]` |
| Employees covered by representatives ≤ total headcount | `[SOC-SAL-REPRESENTES] <= [SOC-EFFECTIF]` |
| Employees below the adequate wage ≤ total headcount | `[SOC-SAL-SOUS-DECENT] <= [SOC-EFFECTIF]` |
| Employees entitled to family leave ≤ total headcount | `[SOC-SAL-CONGES-FAMILLE] <= [SOC-EFFECTIF]` |
| People covered by health and safety ≤ own workforce | `[SOC-SST-COUVERTS] <= [SOC-PERSONNEL]` |
| Fatalities from ill health ≤ work-related fatalities of employees | `[SOC-DECES-MALADIE] <= [SOC-DECES]` |
| Fatalities from ill health ≤ cases of ill health | `[SOC-DECES-MALADIE] <= [SOC-MALADIES-PRO]` |
| Invoices paid within the terms ≤ invoices paid | `[GOV-FACTURES-A-TEMPS] <= [GOV-FACTURES]` |
| Amount paid within the terms ≤ amount paid | `[GOV-FACTURES-MONTANT-A-TEMPS] <= [GOV-FACTURES-MONTANT]` |
